Just as the *Vedas* are called desire trees, you were also like the desire tree. Everyone who ever talked to you walked away satisfied. Like Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu, you converted so many Māyāvādīs to the path of devotional service to Lord Kṛṣṇa. Ṭhākura Bhaktivinoda has said that a Vaiṣṇava is recognized by his preaching work. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have lifted thousands of *mlecchas* like myself to the elevated standard of devotional service. While preaching in India, I meet so many people who recall the wonderful way you inspired them to serve your beloved Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the real *paramahaṁsa*. You had complete faith in the instructions of your spiritual master. You struggled for twenty-five years to establish this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement on this planet. This is the only movement that can save humanity from going to hell. By your personal example, you showed how a devotee should always be involved in reducing the suffering of the living entity. Even when the doctors advised you to take rest, you never stopped preaching.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you exhibited all the twenty-six qualities of a devotee. You were always merciful, humble, truthful, equal to all, faultless, magnanimous, mild and clean. You did not have any material possessions. Whatever donations you received were used to build projects in India. You performed welfare work for everyone. You were always peaceful. Once when you had gone to lecture at a Christian school in Montreal, one Indian gentleman insulted you verbally. But you did not get disturbed—*śānta*. You were completely surrendered to Kṛṣṇa and desireless. You were indifferent to material acquisitions and fixed in devotional service. You had complete control over the six bad qualities. You ate as much as required to keep body and soul together. You were not inebriated. You were always respectful, grave, compassionate and without false prestige. You were friendly, poetic, expert and silent.

Like Lord Caitanya, you have instructed your followers to increase the number of Vaiṣṇavas. You wanted your followers to preach the importance of human life throughout the world. You wanted us to enlighten the people. The devotees of Lord Caitanya are like clouds who pour water that nourishes the fruit of love of God. Presently, the eternal occupation of most of the population is being misused. It is the business of the saintly person to help reestablish the principles of religion. The greatest service one can render to the Lord is to try to infuse devotional service in the heart of the conditioned soul so that he may be released from conditional life. In the *Ādi-līlā*, it is said, “My dear child, continue dancing, chanting and performing *saṅkīrtana*in association with devotees. Furthermore, go out and preach the value of chanting *kṛṣṇa-nāma*, for by this process You will be able to deliver all fallen souls.” Śrīla Prabhupāda, I know this is also your desire. On this auspicious day, I beg to pray for your causeless mercy so that I may be fixed in executing your divine instructions and help propagate the *saṅkīrtana*movement. 108 glories to the wandering mendicant and topmost swanlike devotee, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

*Your eternal servant,*  
Gopāla Kṛṣṇa Goswami

## Haṁsadūta Swami

My lord and master, Śrīla Prabhupāda  
By your sweet will you did depart  
After helping earth off to a start  
To find the Lord within the heart.

Of all the sinners on the earth  
I really feel I am the worst.  
Perhaps that’s why you picked me first  
To help you quench their spiritual thirst.

Since you’ve left I’ve deviated,  
Your foundation underrated.  
The books you carefully narrated  
I dared to think might be outdated

We don’t know who or what we are,  
So you didn’t let me get too far,  
Chasing thoughts of mind bizarre;  
Your mercy keeps me struck in awe.

Fixed up in my little nook  
I sometimes like to sing and cook,  
No more interest to hear or look  
Except for ways to distribute your books.

Materially I’ve died long ago  
Spiritually you’re watching, I know  
Your worst disciple just a show  
How can you (a swan) accept me (a crow)?

*Your humble servant,*  
Haṁsadūta Swami

## Harikeśa Swami

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracārine*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

My dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my most humble obeisances thrown in the dust of your lotus feet.

By Kṛṣṇa’s extraordinary arrangement, the offering I sent for your 1982 Vyāsa-pūjā book was somehow lost in the mail. Thus I have had to telex a very late offering which will by necessity have to be shorter than usual. This is also the mercy of the Lord, since my offerings are traditionally verbose. Factually, this year I have a rather limited realization and vision. I can only see four simple points, and if I perform my service according to these points with great enthusiasm, then I will actually glorify your lotus feet. I must endeavor to please you by …

(1) Printing all your books in all the languages of the world (when no one else is already engaged in that language). We have already finished your books in German, and the Swedish printing is close behind, as are many of the Eastern European languages.

(2) Distributing all these books as you would have wanted, as straightforwardly as possible, without any unnecessary compromises.

(3) Preaching everywhere and constantly, every night if at all possible, and making many new devotees and sympathizers (especially through the radio and our magazines).

(4) Establishing the *varṇāśrama* system, especially the seed of the *varṇāśrama* system, which is the creation of first-class farm communities, as you so much desired.

I am very inspired to do this work. Through your mercy it will gradually succeed. Your desire reigns supreme in my life, your will is my command; it must be fulfilled with all power and enthusiasm. Your glories should be known throughout the world. This whole planet is heading hell-bent towards great chaos and destruction. Any fool can see this. They are ready to reap the *karma* of their enormously sinful actions. By your mercy some small percentage of these persons can have their eyes opened and can accept the nectarean words of your books. I pray to be the simple instrument in this process. I am your eternal servant. Please keep me forever engaged in your transcendental mission.

*Your most foolish servant, waiting outside your door for some scraps of mercy,*  
Harikeśa Swami

## Hṛdayānanda dāsa Goswami

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracārine*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

In the Vedic literature it is said,

*yasya deve parā bhaktir*  
*yathā deve tathā gurau*  
*tasyaite kathitā hy arthāḥ*  
*prakāśante mahātmanaḥ*

Only unto a great soul who has unflinching faith in the Supreme Personality of Godhead and the bona fide spiritual master are all the purports of the Vedic literature automatically revealed. Lord Kṛṣṇa states in the *Bhagavad-gītā* that He cannot be known as He actually is merely by austerity, charity, philosophical speculation or any other procedure except loving devotional service. Similarly, one cannot obtain the mercy of the bona fide spiritual master by any process except humble and loving service. But such loving service must be executed exactly according to the order of the spiritual master. If the spiritual master asks for water one should not bring milk. Similarly, the bona fide disciple should spread the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement exactly according to the orders and example of His Divine Grace Oṁ Viṣṇupada Paramahaṁsa Parivrājakācārya Aṣṭottara-śata Śrī Śrīmad A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda. Such strict obedience is the preliminary basis of love. Śrīla Prabhupāda once took the part of a Christian and argued with some of his disciples that actually Christianity was as bona fide as Kṛṣṇa consciousness. In this conversation, recently printed in *Back to Godhead,* Prabhupāda’s disciples could not defeat his claim that the Christians also loved God until one disciple mentioned that the Christians were not obeying God. Śrīla Prabhupāda accepted this point as undefeatable. Without obedience there is no question of love. One cannot imagine a particular kind of service but must offer to the spiritual master exactly what he has requested. Thus love of Śrīla Prabhupāda begins by chanting sixteen rounds and following the four regulative principles. Without strictly adhering to these points our love remains on the neophyte platform. One who loves Śrīla Prabhupāda will also dedicate his life to spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world in cooperation with Śrīla Prabhupāda’s society, the International Society for Krishna Consciousness. Cooperation with ISKCON is another element of love for Prabhupāda, since Śrīla Prabhupāda fervently desired that His movement remain united. Undoubtedly life within any society involves give-and-take and obedience to certain rules which may not always give one all the advantages one may desire in a specific situation. But the overwhelming advantage of pleasing Śrīla Prabhupāda and remaining united will always keep a true loving disciple of Śrīla Prabhupāda fixed in loyalty to Prabhupāda’s society.

Śrīla Prabhupāda said many times that he had created the entire structure of ISKCON and now we simply had to fill in the structure. Śrīla Prabhupāda did not mention the appendage of a different structure. Thus love of Śrīla Prabhupāda also means the sublime realization that all of Prabhupāda’s programs are as deep as the ocean and each one, carried to completion, can change the history of the world. Kirtanānanda Swami is demonstrating the potency of a farm project carried to great heights. Other empowered Vaiṣṇavas such as Bhagavan Goswami are demonstrating the overwhelming power of book distribution carried to a high level. Śrīla Prabhupāda wanted to spiritualize every significant aspect of human civilization, including theater, education, restaurants, science, literature, government and many others. Those who love Śrīla Prabhupāda should situate themselves in one or more of these authorized programs and develop it to the highest possible degree. Śrīla Prabhupāda is the most intelligent, the most creative and the most inclusive. Therefore any sincere devotee of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, no matter how intelligent, imaginative or expert he may be, can be completely satisfied in transcendental bliss by expanding the programs left by Śrīla Prabhupāda. Although I am disqualified in many ways, I am also trying to carry out this principle and I am receiving the blessings of Lord Kṛṣṇa by the mercy of Śrīla Prabhupāda.

An intelligent person knows that real happiness, real success, real intelligence, and real love of Kṛṣṇa are obtained through the dust of the lotus feet of Śrīla Prabhupāda. We can obtain that dust on our heads by strict obedience to Śrīla Prabhupāda’s order. If we expect his mercy without obeying him, then we are developing *pāpa-buddhi,* or a sinful mentality. We should not sentimentally think that we can disobey the order of Śrīla Prabhupāda and then obtain his mercy by mere sentiment. This mentality is especially dangerous when it involves sinful activities. *Nāmno balād yasya hi pāpa-buddhih.* If one tries to enjoy sense gratification in this world, confident that “My *guru* is very merciful, if I cry certainly he will forgive me,” then surely we are committing the greatest offense against the lotus feet of the spiritual master, and our spiritual position will be most precarious, and possibly ruined. Therefore, remembrance of Śrīla Prabhupāda, love of Śrīla Prabhupāda, glorification of Śrīla Prabhupāda, all must take place on the platform of obedience to Śrīla Prabhupāda. Otherwise our position will be superficial, hypocritical, insubstantial and bereft of the potency needed to fulfill Śrīla Prabhupāda’s dream and desire of making this world Kṛṣṇa conscious.

Personally my only hope is the statement in *śāstra* that one who, although continually suffering the reactions of his past sinful activities, continues to humbly offer obeisances at the lotus feet of *guru* and Kṛṣṇa, certainly becomes eligible for liberation. I am certainly bereft of the exalted qualities of devotees, and due to my low birth it is very difficult for me to understand Kṛṣṇa consciousness, but still I continually offer obeisances at the lotus feet of Śrīla Prabhupāda and strictly carry out his order, and thus I am confident that despite all obstacles in the near future I will go back home, back to Godhead, by the potency of the dust of the lotus feet of Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Hṛdayānanda dāsa Goswami

## Jagadīśa Goswami

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my prostrated obeisances at your lotus feet.

The bona fide spiritual master speaks to his disciple on behalf of Lord Kṛṣṇa and therefore must be regarded by the disciple as directly Lord Kṛṣṇa: *sākṣād-dharitvena samasta-śāstraiḥ.* One who has unflinching faith in the orders of such a *guru* knows everything and is therefore perfectly competent to be an effective instrument in carrying out Lord Kṛṣṇa’s will. You told us many times that your only qualification, and the secret of your tremendous success in establishing the International Society for Krishna Consciousness, is your strict adherence to the order of your Guru Mahārāja. We know, of course, that your strict adherence to Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta’s order is the product of your purity *(vyavasāyātmikā buddhir ekeha kuru-nandana),* which indicates your exalted spiritual position in the first place.

But I am a wretched fellow, a scoundrel and a fool, yet there seems to be some desire in me to be your one-hundred-percent-obedient servant. Please bless me that that desire will become strong, stronger, strongest, so that the multitude of other desires which are implanted in my heart will become burned to ashes, and so that I may actually do some service for you and your Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement.

*Your eternal servant,*  
Jagadīśa Goswami

## Jagajīvana Goswami

This struggling life  
A world of strife  
Life’s plans all gone askew

Then by chance  
From māyās glance  
We leave and come to you.

And in that dawn  
Of your sweet song  
I hope will come to pass

That some day soon  
I get the boon  
Of serving you at last.

O Prabhupāda,  
My darkened heart  
Cries out to be with you.

My life, my soul  
My everything  
Condemned. What can I do?

But if I do  
As you have done  
Remembering what you’ve told

I’ll be with you  
And serve you, too,  
And heal this sickened soul.

Jagajīvana Goswami

## Jayapatāka Swami

What would we be? Alone and lost  
Your mercy, kind, upon us came.  
From dark to light, “A fool made wise.”  
Your wonder touch—transforms the heart.  
You gave us life! Your Divine Grace,  
Your vision broad. Life is so short,  
Keep us near you. Your lotus feet,  
Your word our home—light in the dark.

You’ve made the house. Home for the world.  
ISKCON your body. All are your parts.  
Work together, the path is clear.  
Follow Kṛṣṇa! You lead the way.  
Doubt the order—then lose the way.  
Listen to māyā—be led astray.  
Your words so clear, love our brothers.  
All are your parts. ISKCON your body.

Your Divine Grace has braved the storms.  
Crossed the oceans. No help at hand  
Guru’s order—your strength and force,  
Kṛṣṇa’s kind glance—beside your side.  
Give us your strength—to follow you.  
Give us patience—to tread your path.  
Give us your shade—from lust and greed.  
You by our side—we stand in truth.

Your mercy great. Your vision wide.  
Caitanya’s mercy—personified.  
Without Gaur’s glance—to preach no chance.  
You are our link—always our guide.  
The world comes first—your words so strong.  
The weight to bear—your children all.  
Prasādam to give—the dhāmas to serve,  
A mercy gift—Your Divine Grace.

The ocean deep—with whales and sharks.  
Without your grace—a fearful place.  
Alone we die. United live.  
Humbly serve you—through GBC.  
Śrī Siddhānta Sarasvatī  
also Ṭhākura Bhaktivinod  
ordered to serve under GBC.  
Work to preserve Gaur’s sacred work.

“Half work is done—half left to be.”  
Saṅkīrtana, books, temples and farms.  
You’ve done the job. Now we must act.  
Preserve what’s now. Expand beyond.  
All the buildings, and all the men—  
Everything’s yours! All by your grace!  
Work we must do. Preach and increase.  
Raise “Kṛṣṇa” sun—darkness will flee.

Man in the dark—“Work like a dog”;  
Thinks he’s his shell—living in hell.  
Man thinks he’s God. “Master of all,”  
“Merge in the light”—darkest of all!  
Man thinks he’s great—“servant of God.”  
Skips the guru—no love of God.  
Your Divine Grace—gives preaching mood.  
Mood of gopīs, real love for God.

This life so short, obstacles great.  
I’m so fallen. No hope but you.  
Fiercely cling to—your lotus feet.  
Cross the ocean—return to you.  
Give your mercy—to my children.  
They advance well, Your Divine Grace.  
Serving ISKCON, serving your word.  
Preaching glory—around the world.

*Your insignificant servant,*  
Jayapatāka Swami

## Kīrtanānanda Swami

*śrī-guru karuṇā-sindhu, adhama janāra bandhu,*  
*lokanāth lokera jīvana*  
*hā hā prabhu koro doyā, deho more pada-chāyā,*  
*ebe jaśa ghuṣuk tribhuvana*

My dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the supreme spiritual master. No one can be equal to you. Your mercy has inundated the whole world with the nectarean ocean of love of Kṛṣṇa. Therefore you alone are qualified to be the teacher of everyone. Your words alone are the life and soul of the devotees. O master, be merciful unto me; give me the shade of your lotus feet. I am tired and weak, and I have no other shelter than you. May your glories now be proclaimed throughout the three worlds. You are the sum total of all the demigods; therefore if you empower me, I can do anything. Please allow me to spread your holy name all over the world. You can do that, for that will please Kṛṣṇa.

Kīrtanānanda Swami

## Pañcadraviḍa Swami

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracārine*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Śrīla Prabhupāda, Your Love Is Fathomless

Now we are sailing in your ship of devotional service,  
And you, a kind captain, are also possessed of fathomless love.  
Were we not aboard your boat, drowned we would be,  
Down, down to the very bottom of māyā’s black, churning sea.

But māyā, with her tossing, frothing waves,  
Cannot disturb your golden ship as it plies its way  
To the waiting shores of Kṛṣṇa’s waiting land.  
“Land ho! Goloka Vṛndāvana ki jaya!” shouts a Vaikuṇṭha man,  
And the effulgent captain stretches his upraised hands  
And dances, as we, amazed, do stand  
Marveling at his wondrous love and his great compassion.

The conditioned souls cannot encompass your devotional ocean  
Because they are all so many frogs, stuck in their deep wells.  
Of the Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu they have no notion,  
But if but a darting word of your books can penetrate their false-ego shells  
Then a drop of that bhakti can enter and be tasted by their parched souls,  
And they can leap free of their dark, damp prison holes.

And happily following the lotus feet of you, our captain,  
We enter into your seaworthy vessel.  
The bells are ringing, an arāti has begun,  
The breeze is blowing, the conch is sounding,  
The ship is moving, the Deities are glistening.  
And you are welcoming us all, calling us your true sons;  
And linked with you, together we are chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa.

Nobody can estimate the gift that Śrīla Prabhupāda has given us;  
No one can begin to understand his moist devotional heart;  
No one walking in this mortal world can ever replace him;  
No servant of Kṛṣṇa is so dear as our only father.  
No child can feel so protected as one who is  
Locked in the embrace of Prabhupāda’s loving arms;  
No greater mercy can be bestowed.

He is our waiting, loving father, dearmost friend, eternal companion  
Who has never forgotten us.  
And he is living in his words, his instructions.  
My brothers, do not turn away. Strive to be  
United, run to serve him, and on this Vyāsa-pūjā day  
Dance together in the dust of his lotus feet.

*Śrīla Prabhupāda, I pray to always remain your eternal servant,*  
Pañcadraviḍa Swami

## Prabhupāda-kṛpa Goswami

**A** bhay meaning fearless, no thought of any defeat,  
**C** haran, having full shelter at Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet.  
**B** haktivedanta indicates your knowledge culminates in love,  
**H** are is whom you represent to reach the Lord above.  
**A** cintya-bhedābheda-tattva, inconceivable philosophy,  
**K** ṛṣṇa and His devotees, you have made reality.  
**T** attva you have shown as three features although one,  
**I** mpersonal and Paramātmā, partial aspects of Bhagavān.  
**V** ṛndāvana land of touchstone, by your devotion manifest,  
**E** cstatic land of Kṛṣṇa, now appearing in the West.  
**D** harma means to act as Kṛṣṇa wants us to,  
**A** bsolute surrender, which is personified in you.  
**N** itya, the eternal nature, you can bestow because you are  
**T** ri-kāla-jña, knower of past, present and future.  
**Ā** nanda, very blissful, always happy from within,  
**S** wami, sense controller, completely free from sin.  
**V** iśuddha, purified goodness, beyond the realm of piety,  
**A** cyuta, your godly nature, of infallibility.  
**M** āyā is your servant, on you she cannot act,  
**I** śvaraḥ paramaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ you established as a fact.  
**Ś** rīla means you’re beautiful, the friend of all you meet,  
**R** asa, the taste of devotion, you make unlimitedly sweet.  
**Ī** ncarnation of the mercy of Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi,  
**L** iberating millions to the world beyond the sky.  
**A** vatāra descended, producing literatures by the score,  
**P** reaching is your mission, books distributed by the crore.  
**R** enounced of all self-interest, guru’s order to fulfill,  
**A** ctions speaking louder than words, leaving others standing still.  
**B** est of all the Vaiṣṇavas, you stand higher than the rest,  
**H** are Kṛṣṇa is a household word from your preaching in the West.  
**U** ttama mahā-bhāgavata, you are the leader of your brothers,  
**P** rabhupāda is your name, to be worshiped by all others.  
**Ā** dau śraddhā is the basis on which spiritual life depends,  
**D** evotion to your lotus feet means materialism ends.  
**A** ll glories, all glories, all glories to His Divine Grace  
**A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Śrīla Prabhupāda!**

*Your humble and fallen servant,*  
Prabhupāda-kṛpa Goswami

## Rameśvara Swami

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my prostrate obeisances at your lotus feet. As we have been praying every day, so today on your Vyāsa-pūjā festival we especially pray that Kṛṣṇa may empower us not to deviate one inch from your service. The greatest benediction of the Supreme Lord is that we lowly conditioned souls have been allowed to render you direct service. Those who have not tasted the nectar of serving your divine mission, which is nondifferent from serving your very self, are still attracted by the material forms of money and sex and a life of simply eating and sleeping. But by becoming attached to serving you, one develops a drop of your transcendental qualities, which are manifest as deep as a fathomless ocean. Even a drop of your transcendental qualities and preaching potency is more than enough to guarantee liberation from this material world. So what to speak of the fortune of those who are so attached to serving you that their service has become much more dear than life itself?

By Lord Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s causeless compassion, you appeared as the personification of the Lord’s mercy and love for the fallen souls. In serving you, we have begun to know the highest transcendental bliss—ecstatic love for the devotees of the Lord! You are the perfect, unparalleled *ācārya,* showing your perfect devotion for your spiritual master’s mission. As we watched you renounce all personal comforts and tirelessly exhibit superhuman energy for translating, publishing and distributing your perfect and complete transcendental literatures, and then push yourself to travel to the most remote areas of the world to preach, and then engage your disciples in the same way, we could understand the real path of ecstatic service to Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

You are irreplaceable to us. We hanker to always be in your presence as your servants. In separation we are endeavoring to keep the same mood of *saṅkīrtana* and book distribution that attracted you so much when you were present. We must desperately try to establish in our temples and in our own hearts this *saṅkīrtana* mood, so that we may hope to continue to transcendentally attract you to giving us your personal association. Only through this intense preaching spirit and book distribution can we be assured of perceiving and feeling your presence, and our goal is to go on feeling your presence guiding us at every moment, day and night. This would be the ultimate benediction for us.

To meditate on your pastimes of preaching and renunciation, your sacrifices and preaching mood, we can easily realize how different you are from all other Vaiṣṇavas. We are so attracted by your divine preaching *līlā* that we fallen souls also aspire to dedicate ourselves in a similar way. We beg from the core of our hearts to also sacrifice our personal comforts, our sleep, our family life—just to increase the publishing and distributing of your books, the making of new devotees and the opening of temples in all the remote towns and villages *of* the world. For us, the highest experience *of* ecstatic love for you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, is in sacrificing everything to push on the *saṅkīrtana* movement you have brought. If Lord Kṛṣṇa is at all pleased with any service we might have rendered, and we may thus pray for some benediction, then we pray with all our might to increase our sacrifice thousands and millions of times more, to exert ourselves to the fullest extent for your ISKCON movement, and renounce the last traces of personal consideration and comfort. By your causeless mercy we pray for a burning desire to give your books to every soul in the world. We hanker to taste the ecstasy *of* this *saṅkīrtana* mood, to be consumed in the fire *of saṅkīrtana* day and night without deviation. Please, please accept us in this way.

*We beg to remain your eternal saṅkīrtana servants, birth after birth,*  
Rāmeśvara Swami

## Rūpānuga dāsa

My dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my most humble obeisances.

You gave me revelations to last many lifetimes. Seeing the Absolute Truth would change any man’s life, but not mine! Still I cling to my pleasures, avoiding the taste of the holy name, knowing full well that to taste transcendental nectar will only bring death to my material life. So I avoid it, steadfastly. You said I was steadfast, but that is my steadfastness.

You gave me revelations to last many lifetimes. More than enough to convince any doubtful soul, but not me! Still reluctant on the path of surrender, fearing it will lead to the destruction of my attachments, I hold up the hopes of material happiness. You said to go on in my enthusiastic way, but that is my enthusiasm.

You gave me revelations to last many lifetimes. I knew people would come to you by the millions. Such knowledge would have humbled any man at your lotus feet, but not me. Still I flex my puny intelligence, straining to predict something or to say something new, foreseeing that to be only a transparent medium would require control of my free will. You called me a preacher, but that is my teaching.

You gave me revelations to last many lifetimes, but now I am simply trying to remember the times when I was the most Kṛṣṇa conscious. I cannot utter a word without hearing your gorgeous voice or speak anything without first reading your perfect words. So independent in your presence, now I have become your helpless dependent. All I can do is meditate upon your words: “We do not claim to be perfect; but we do not cheat anyone.”

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, *ācārya* of the Brahma-Madhva-Gauḍīya-sampradāya—you who had firm faith in the words of your beloved Guru Mahārāja! By that faith you have saved the whole world for ten thousand years. Thank you forever for sharing that faith with us, and now by your mercy only can we distribute that faith to others.

*Your most fallen disciple,*  
Rūpānuga dāsa

## Satsvarūpa dāsa Goswami

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Now as they define  
how the jīva came  
from the brahma-jyotir  
ftom the lower rasa  
and by guru’s grace  
he’s finally to return  
to eternal home  
with Kṛṣṇa—  
when I hear it  
I think  
I want my rasa with you.

I don’t care that there  
are higher understandings,  
I want my rasa with you.

I want to serve you  
and this time  
do it right.

Where you are—  
are you preaching?  
Is it right for me  
to think this?  
I know there are many  
who are your men,  
I am in their number  
not exclusive, not the best,  
but you know me also;  
take me to you,  
that’s my request.

**A Prayer to Begin the Fifth Volume of Śrīla Prabhupāda-līlāmṛta**

Spiritual master, you are like a distant star to me; I am trying to follow your inconceivable orbit in the years of your ascending the dark sky of Kali-yuga.

You moved auspiciously through the heavens, 1974, ’75, ’76, ’77—please allow me to be with you again and through me grant the vision of your *līlā* to the world.

I, a lost soul, desire to realize my mission of tracing your path, and traveling to be with you. I must go at all costs, but I pray to you to lift me there.

Your life is a Milky Way—so many glorious heavenly bodies mix that I cannot count them all, and each one is an almost unreachable mystery.

By your grace I have already been granted the gift of subtle travel to the planets of your *līlā* and seen there your humanlike activities, beneficial for the people of the universe, and I have described it.

There is no end to the orbiting of your life. And now I desire to go again-please take me further than ever before, to those worlds where you enact the preacher’s mission.

We yearn to behold you again, preaching the message of Lord Caitanya, dictating your books, moving with infinite mercy throughout the continents of this earth.

Although you are fixed eternally as a star in the pastimes of Lord Kṛṣṇa and Caitanya Mahāprabhu, you simultaneously appear before us as the incarnation of the devotee in 1970. Please come into my vision in that form, as the saffron preacher.

The world was sunk in the Garbhodaka Ocean, and you dove, O *bhāgavata* preacher, to the bottom, and brought her up again, carrying her on the tusks of your preaching. You killed all the demons and left behind staunch followers to carry on the mission of Lord Caitanya.

You are as exalted as Vaikuṇṭha’s majesty,  
and you sit humbly,  
and you preach as lion-guru,  
and you move as jet-age mendicant  
and set the pace  
for ten thousand years  
of devotional life on earth.

Whether we fail like Jaṭāyu  
or succeed like Arjuna,  
there is only victory  
if you allow us to engage  
in your service.  
Let the Līlāmṛta shower  
like meteorites of mercy  
on the suffering, demon-gripped earth,  
and please grant me entry  
into your līlā.

Satsvarūpa dāsa Goswami

## Tamāla Kṛṣṇa Goswami

**Śrīla Prabhupāda’s “Universal Form”**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

A few years ago, at the time of your departure, I became bewildered, overwhelmed by the loss of your personal association. In those months before your leaving, your disciples stayed with you practically throughout the twenty-four hours of each day. When you finally departed, it seemed that our will to live went with you. We had to consciously decide whether to remain lost in lamentation or to seek your continued association by remembering your instructions. It was an all-important decision which each disciple had to make. Some of us had intimate, extensive association, while others had never personally met you. But through your letters, news of your activities, and the ongoing publication of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*,we were very much aware of your presence. Now there was none of these to remind us of you.

In retrospect, we are now able to observe the results according to how each disciple made his or her decision. Those who were intelligent faithfully served your instructions and continued to prosper, while those less fortunate have gone astray from the path, no longer able to see that you are still leading us, just as before.

I must thank you. You have been most kind to me, for despite my many disqualifications, you have allowed me to perceive your continued presence in no way diminished by your physical departure. As previously, when you liberally gave me your personal association, once again you have appeared before me, but this time in another form. I now see you manifest as an ever- increasing, worldwide society of Kṛṣṇa devotees.

Within that form I see people of all races. The Africans and Chinese are the bottoms of your feet, and your heels are those born of the aboriginal tribes. Your toes are the Arabs and devotees of sundry lands. Your ankles are the devotees of Eastern Europe and Russia. The Australians are your knees, while the devotees from Europe and the Americas, your right and left leg respectively. The Indians form your waist.

Your head is the preachers, your arms the temple presidents, your waist the farmers, and those who perform labor are your feet. Your chest is the *sannyāsīs, brahmacārīs,*and *vānaprasthas*.The householders who conceive children according to religious regulations are your genitals.

Your front side is the totality of dutiful service rendered by your sincere followers, while your back side is those shameless devotees who have given up your service. These are of three types. Those who due to weakness fall down to sense gratification form your upper back. Those led astray by *sahajiyā* and impersonal philosophies constitute your lower back. All those who are envious, and who try to find fault with you, who are beyond any fault, are the lowest of all and are the rectum of their spiritual master. Criticizing your movement, ISKCON, which is your body, they try to directly harm and even destroy it.

In your right eye I see Kṛṣṇa, while in your left eye everything else in relationship to Him. Your ears are the attentive reception of Kṛṣṇa’s glories chanted by pure devotees. The aristocratic, backward tilt of your head, and your broad, flaring nostrils indicate aloofness and disdain for things that are mundane.

Your noble bearing is the pride a Vaiṣṇava feels in serving Kṛṣṇa exclusively; your strong walking gait, the irresistible progress of the *saṅkīrtana* movement.

Your *kaṇṭhi-mālā* represents fealty to *guru,* your sacred thread the brahminical culture. The two vertical lines of your *tilaka* are equal dependence upon *guru* and Kṛṣṇa. The dots of sandalwood pulp which adorn your forehead symbolize the twenty-six saintly qualities decorating the personality of a Vaiṣṇava.

Your outer garments are the variegated programs for spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness, and the hidden activities in the communist countries are your underclothes.

Your garland of pure white gardenias represents the all-attractive nature of devotional service. The bees that surround this garland are the devotees allured by the highly-scented flavor of your all-attractive devotion to Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

ISKCON is your body, the GBC your will. The BBT is your heart. Your transcendental books are the blood flowing from the heart, while *lakṣmī* is the blood that returns.

Your mind is discretion for discerning spirit from matter. Your intelligence is wisdom which sees everything in connection to Kṛṣṇa. And your ego is our true identity as Kṛṣṇa’s eternal servants.

Vṛndāvana is your residence, Bombay your office, and Māyāpur your place of worship.

Enthusiasm is your boyhood, determination your youth, while patience is your old age. The movements of your gigantic body mark the passage of time spanning ten thousand years.

The *ārati* ceremony is your joyous dance of devotion. *Ācamana* is the purifying process of *sādhana*, while incense is the fragrant perspiration produced during the rhythmic movements of your dance. The offering of camphor, fresh like morning dew, is the awakening of spontaneous *bhakti*; the glowing flames of the ghee lamp, your pure, spotless love for Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa. The conchshell filled with water is your tears of ecstasy which you wipe away and try to conceal with the cloth of humility. The aromatic, multicolored offering of flowers is varieties of ecstatic symptoms which erupt upon your transcendental body. And with the *camara* and fan you calm yourself, patient in your enduring love for Kṛṣṇa. The sound of the conchshell heralds the victory of your unrivaled devotion, which has succeeded in conquering the Lord, who is normally never to be conquered.

O my eternal spiritual master, lord of my life, best of friends, father and guide! I bow down to you from in front, from behind, and from all sides. Though you are no longer present before me in the form I so much loved, you have nonetheless given me the eyes to see you manifest everywhere in everything. On this, your Vyāsa-pūjā day, I pray for the benediction that I may continue to behold you in this “universal form,” always present before me even up until my final breath. Then when I give up my body, please bring me to where you are, so that I may once again hear your familiar voice, touch your divine form, and hold your lotus feet upon my heart eternally.

*Your servant,*  
Tamāla Kṛṣṇa Goswami

## Vāsudeva dāsa

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

On this auspicious day of your appearance, I pray that my love and devotion for you will develop and increase unlimitedly, that I never deviate from your instructions.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, I want to simply beg at your feet for the strength and direction to carry on my service to you. Please give me shelter at your lotus feet, even though I have no qualification, for I have no shelter other than your lotus feet. You are the most merciful, and you are the most expert spiritual master, and therefore it is easily within your power to deliver me.

*Your humble servant in Fiji,*  
Vāsudeva dāsa

## Vyāsa-pūjā Homages from ISKCON Centers

## Accra, Ghana

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracārine*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*tvaṁ naḥ sandarśito dhātrā*  
*dustaraṁ nistitīrṣatām*  
*kaliṁ sattva-haraṁ puṁsāṁ*  
*karṇa-dhāra ivārṇavam*

**“We think that we have met Your Goodness by the will of providence, just so that we may accept you as captain of the ship for those who desire to cross the difficult ocean of Kali, which deteriorates all the good qualities of a human being.”** ***(Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **1.1.22)**

The causeless mercy of the spiritual master is unrivaled in the three worlds. The spiritual master, being situated in his eternal *rasa* with Kṛṣṇa, is fully satisfied. But when he sees the Supreme Lord unhappy due to the state of the materially enamored souls, he comes and tries to relieve the Lord’s distress. Coming to this material world, he is placed in so many difficulties, this being the nature of the material world. But he gladly accepts any tribulation with open arms, only caring for the Lord’s smiling face.

Among such spiritual masters you are known as Śrīla Prabhupāda—“master at whose feet all other masters sit.” This statement is supported by your life history. So many years you spent toiling to organize a society for world preaching. During those years, you lived like a beggar, even though comfortable life awaited you. You sacrificed your life by sailing the sea at such an advanced age, and you fulfilled the prophecy of Lord Caitanya, the establishment of a world-wide organization capable of distributing the holy name all over the world.

After Lord Caitanya’s departure, the tidal wave of love of Godhead began to subside. You have risen like the moon and rejuvenated this wave, flooding the material world. Even after your departure, this wave is increasing. That is the proof of your spiritual potency. You are desiring so much the deliverance of the conditioned souls that Kṛṣṇa is empowering ISKCON more and more. Just as the power of a magnet is transferred to an iron object attached to it and it is transferred to the iron attached to that iron also, Kṛṣṇa is transferring His mercy to you, who are so kindly pouring it on us. We are witnessing now the rise of this flood here in West Africa. We are praying that we may be strong instruments in your service. We beg to remain servants of your lotus feet, which are traversing the three worlds. Please accept us as your humble servants.

## Adelaide

The age of Kali has begun. Oh, what a threat to all  
the people of every race.  
Now irreligion is rampant, envy and hatred are rife,  
and no one cares for God’s face.

This life is all in all, they say.  
Enjoy, enjoy, forget what the future can hold.  
By any means, just enjoy this day.

Yes, Kali has them in his grip,  
feeding them all kinds of horrible things.  
And they are thinking, “Yes, it’s just like gold.

But so fortunate it is that the Lord did not forsake us.  
He sent His most dear servant with the cure,  
with the needed message, that material happiness is  
just not able to compare to spiritual rasa.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you were like a physician because we were  
all diseased and you had to make an incision.  
Yet you were the most expert, for we felt no pain,  
and on the contrary, we became sane.

How can we enumerate your glories to sufficient height?  
You have done more than them all,  
because we have gotten back our sight.

Now it just remains for us to stay at your side,  
to espouse the benefits of your sweet confidence.  
On this Vyāsa-pūjā day may we offer you our most humble  
obeisances at your feet.

*Your disciples and granddisciples in Adelaide, South Australia*

## Argentina

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracārine*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

Again the most auspicious day of your appearance has come, and again we realize how little love and devotion we actually have for you. Even though we become very emotionally overwhelmed by remembering you, still, looking at how much service we have done, we become ashamed. That is the real test. If we really loved you, we would try to fulfill your heart’s desire to make this world more and more Kṛṣṇa conscious. You have shown us the perfect example by serving your Guru Mahārāja without a tinge of selfish desire and with great determination. We know there is no excuse—we are the only ones to blame. Even though you apparently left us, you are still ready to give us your association in so many ways to guide us. If we would just want to accept it. But dull as we are, we avoid you; we still do not take your instructions seriously enough, and thereby we waste valuable time. And even though we have acted like this, offending you and your sincere disciples, still you have tolerated us and did not send us away. O Prabhupāda, this compassion we do not comprehend has made us incredibly indebted to you! We know that it is our only hope, and that we must under no circumstances lose it. Otherwise our lives are useless, and we do not know where to go.

We feet very lowly that on this day we only have empty words to offer you. But we hope that with your blessings we will be able to distribute your books again soon and preach more openly, as Lord Caitanya’s movement right now is still prohibited in this country. In this way we will also be able to offer you something more substantial.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

We beg to remain your eternal servants,

## Athens

*śrī-guru-caraṇa-padma, kevala-bhakati-sadma,*  
*bando mui sāvadhāna mate*

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracārine*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We offer our most respectful and humble obeisances in the holy dust of your lotus feet. We hope that while we endeavor to serve and glorify you, some of that dust might stick on our heads. In this way we shall become eligible to reenter the spiritual world. The more we try to serve you, the more we understand the unlimited qualities of Kṛṣṇa and His devotional service, and we realize simultaneously that those qualities were always manifest in your divine personality. We enjoy appreciating these qualities, especially because they are always increasing, expanding, manifesting newly. And we enjoy glorifying this unlimited greatness that makes you the most attractive, loving and powerful person we know.

We see others in this world acting, grossly or subtly, to gain respect and service from others, but in fact they are only servants of their own senses, and they want others to serve their senses also. So the end result is always pain, fear, frustration and rejection. No one finds their relationship with their wife, friend, relative, pet, etc., completely satisfying; and no one finds ultimate satisfaction with the political or government leader, with the teacher or professor, with the doctor or scientist, or even with the so-called *guru* or *svāmī.* Everyone is self-interested, so where is the question of satisfying someone else? But you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are not self-interested—you are interested only in Kṛṣṇa’s (and everyone else’s) satisfaction. You are completely satisfied due to your loving relationship with Kṛṣṇa, and you have no need or desire for service or respect from others. And yet when people relate with you, they become completely satisfied, free from all selfish motivations. They become pure, happy, and magnanimous souls.

At the annual Māyāpur-Vṛndāvana festival we see our fellow devotees whom we have not seen for a year or two or three, or even more, and we are joyfully astounded at how they have become so much more bright-faced and jolly, effulgent and powerful, serious and fixed. The proof is there for anyone who cares to see: the result of contacting Your Divine Grace is that one contacts Kṛṣṇa, the supreme pure, the reservoir of all wonderful qualities. And there is no end to it!

Therefore we are feeling a little incapable of glorifying Your Divine Grace. However, we are sure that you will accept and appreciate any sincere offering we present, however insignificant it may be, because we know that you have great compassion and love for us, and we also have great love for you.

Everything we do is insignificant, but you always tolerate our contamination. Like a loving, compassionate father you always accept our foolish endeavors to serve and please you! Therefore, and for no other reason, we have understood something about the mission of our lives, about our relationships with you and Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

As we attempt to serve your instructions, we feet naturally inclined to glorify you; in fact, the result of our endeavoring is that you are glorified automatically. As explained in the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta*, *Ādi-līlā*, the order of the spiritual master carries with it the power to carry out that order; so when we make even some small attempt to seriously obey you, wonderful results are achieved. Many people become attracted to your devotees and to our movement, large sums of money become available for expanding Kṛṣṇa consciousness, huge properties are acquired for Kṛṣṇa conscious farms and temples, hundreds of beautiful and impressive literatures are published and distributed all over the planet! When people see these things, they wonder, “How is it all possible? Why is it happening? Who is behind it? He must be a special person!” And we answer, “Yes, he *is* a special person. He is a *very* special person!” So, Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your own mercy and potency, we get another blissful opportunity to introduce your glorious self to another forgetful conditioned spirit soul! We always like to glorify your transcendental qualities more and more.

On this especially significant day of your appearance on this planet, we pray to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that we may increase our service to you and Lord Kṛṣṇa by better understanding your instructions to us. Because of these instructions, everything is going on so perfectly, and even the greatest, seemingly overwhelming odds are transcended! (As our movement grows, so also do the tests.) We see that you understood everything from the beginning, including everything we would need to know in the future. As we come to struggle and face particular problems, we see that if we had understood your original instructions we would not have been disturbed in such situations. Because we are not appreciating enough your position and instructions, sometimes we lose the opportunity to preach, or we even risk the reputation of your ISKCON movement. Please, therefore, Śrīla Prabhupāda, bless us with ever-increasing realization, so that we may develop our serious desire to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness more and more.

The Governing Body Commission is a perfect spiritual instrument which you created, Śrīla Prabhupāda, with your perfect spiritual intelligence, to guide your ISKCON movement through the ocean of faults of this Kali-yuga. Again we must beg your forgiveness for our faithlessness and doubt, for again we had to see the proof ourselves, in the course of time, of the absolute nature of your instructions. But the proof is there, clear and sure—you are present in your GBC; your ISKCON ship cuts through the tidal waves of Kali-yuga’s doubly-strong mayic force. Proof again! Doubts and doubters are exposed; conviction and faith are confirmed; no one can deny; every argument is answered! If the envious dare to vocalize their challenges, we defeat their every attack! Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are with us—if only we could appreciate your association enough, then we would be able to fulfill all your transcendental desires immediately. And we could effectively spread your mission and glories everywhere, very quickly. This will be the perfection of our glorification of you, O beloved spiritual master, O supreme father, friend, and guide! If, through our complete surrender in executing your order, we can establish your mission everywhere, then the glory of your perfect link with Kṛṣṇa will be manifest naturally, and all people will unite under Lord Caitanya’s *saṅkīrtana* banner, enjoying perfect satisfaction within your universal International Society for Krishna Consciousness family!

*The grateful servants of your servants at ISKCON Athens, Greece,*

**A Song for Śrīla Prabhupāda**

Why am I dependent on anyone but you—  
as if there were anyone else  
could pull me through  
this darkness I’ve fallen into but you,

*Śrī Guru!*

How could I think that there’s  
something more to gain,  
when there’s nothing you didn’t  
freely give, nothing you left  
unexplained, and  
I know there is nothing I can do without you,

*Śrī Guru!*

Your mercy is all I am made of;  
forgetting you is all I’m afraid of.  
Let my promise remain  
strong and true, to serve you,

*Śrī Guru!*

‘Though many may fall by the wayside,  
I don’t want to hide  
from the blessings you have  
given me, master! You have  
set me free. Even if I serve you  
eternally, I still will not be able  
to repay you,

*Śrī Guru!*

All glories to you, our spiritual master!  
Delivering us ftom the material disaster,  
we pray to enthuse you, too,

*Śrī Guru!*

## Auckland

**I was born in the darkest ignorance, and my spiritual master, His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, has forced me to open my eyes with the blazing torchlight of transcendental knowledge. I offer my humble obeisances to his lotus feet again and again.**

Forgetfulness or ignorance is the nature of this material existence, and the conditioned soul, due to his association with matter from time immemorial, is prone to come under this influence. Continually rising and failing like the waves of the ocean, the conditioned soul struggles hard to maintain his false position. Unable to achieve his desired goals he is filled with lamentation. Then again, due to forgetfulness, he thinks that somehow or other he can enjoy. Thus this cycle continues practically eternally. One may think, “If only I could be freed from this forgetfulness.” But the fact, however, is that this impurity is not being forced upon us from some external source; rather, it is self-inflicted through the most subtle of all elements—desire. Due to this impure desire to act independently of Kṛṣṇa we forget our blissful, elevated position as a servant of the Lord and thus we suffer under the stringent laws of material nature, sometimes experiencing life as a human, a dog, or even less. Even now, after having taken spiritual initiation from you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, after hearing your clear instructions, after seeing your pure and practical example, after so many so-called realizations—still I am forgetful. I am forgetful of your causeless mercy with which you lifted me from the dirtiest regions of existence. In fact I am ashamed of my forgetfulness. All that I can do now, therefore, is to remember first of all your instructions, then the way you spoke on behalf of Kṛṣṇa: the way you forcefully preached; the way you liked to joke sometimes with the reporters; the way you gently but proudly lifted your head; the way you danced; the way you cried for your Guru Mahārāja; the way that you loved us all. Please let us always remember you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. You are all that we are made of.

*Your disciples and granddisciples,*  
*The servants of Rādhā-Giridhārī at New Varsana*

## Austin

Most beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Under the shelter of your lotus feet we attempt to glorify your transcendental qualities. You are the pure representative of Śrīla Vyāsadeva, Your Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
Your words are like the sun, which eradicates the darkness of illusion. Without your mercy we cannot see our fallen condition.

Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
You are a perfect magician -by following your instructions one can inconceivably attain love for Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
You are our link to Kṛṣṇa consciousness. You have the strength to lift up the conditioned souls from the perils of the age of Kali.

Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
Your glories are endless. Thinking of you is nondifferent from remembering the Supreme Lord.

Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
You present the teachings of the Vedas according to time, place and circumstances. You are the bona fide spiritual master.

Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
Your purity defeats the mentality of “realist” philosophers who say that there never was any one person named Vyāsadeva. The teachings of such doubting impersonalists who think that one man cannot write so many books are proved useless in the light of your powerful translations.

Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
You are steadfast in Kṛṣṇa’s service. You are pleased when someone has even a spark of desire to serve Kṛṣṇa.

Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
You are the lion *guru* who never remains quiet when the Lord or His representative is blasphemed.

Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
You are like the honeybee which tastes the nectar of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s glories; then you give the nectar to everyone through your talks about Kṛṣṇa.

Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
You are always offering four kinds of foodstuffs. You are satisfied when the Lord is properly worshiped.

Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
Service to Your Divine Grace keeps us from wilting away. You protect us as the lotus flower is protected by water from the scorching sun. We need your dear association.

Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
Your *Bhāgavatam*translations keep us intact with the disciplic succession. May we forever remain in the shade of your lotus feet.

Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
The *Vedas* state that whoever has faith in both the spiritual master and the Supreme Lord, to him all the imports of Vedic knowledge are revealed. You are bringing together the blind Western countries and the lame Eastern countries in devotional service.

Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
You are the embodiment of Rādhārāṇī’s mercy. You bring everyone closer to Kṛṣṇa.

Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
You have traveled so far to serve your spiritual master. You know that real home is not anywhere in this temporary world.

Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
You give your disciples the strength to push on the saṅkīrtanamovement. As your humble servants, let us always fulfill your desire to preach.

Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
Let us remember your unlimited pastimes forever. We are made simply of your mercy.

Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
We must reaffirm our full commitment to your divine mission. We must shake off the reluctant mentality and flood the world with love of Godhead.

*Your servants at Austin ISKCON,*  
Caitanyacandra dāsa / Dharmabhāvana dāsa / Pūrṇacandra dāsa / Nārada Muni dāsa / Bhakta Doug / Bhakta Allen / Bhakta Steve / Jaya Govinda-devī dāsī

## Bali

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my most humble obeisances at your holy lotus feet.

Somehow or other I have gotten the inconceivable mercy to be able to preach here in Bali, where there are so many innocent souls to be delivered unto your lotus feet. I am completely unworthy of this opportunity, but it shows as a mark of your immense potency, that you are able to make use of fallen and abominable persons like myself to perform your *līlā* of saving the world by the distribution of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. I hope that by your blessings one day I may become pure, and that until that time I do not do anything to spoil your wonderful preaching work. I hope that my vision may become wide enough to not impede the distribution of your influence, for there is no telling what huge and awe-inspiring changes may take place in this world as a result of your coming. I request that you please keep me on your team in whatever capacity you may deem suitable.

Also, please bless these sweet and budding Vaiṣṇavas here in Bali, that they may become strong and courageous and never deterred by *māyā’s* advances.

*Your most unworthy, aspiring servants,*

## Baltimore

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

We bow to you, Kṛṣṇa’s dear spokesman,  
His feet your shelter, your feet in motion,  
Named after knowledge and devotion,  
The swami who dared to cross the ocean.

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracārine*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Message of Sarasvatī in your hand,  
Preaching Gaurāṅga’s divine command,  
“Chant Hare Kṛṣṇa! Save this land!”  
You kicked on the voidists and foiled their plans.

To those who were listening, you then turned and said,  
“Come, take some prasādam, you all took half dead!  
Spiritual food cures spiritual starvation,  
Mṛdaṅgas and karatālas aid recuperation.

“Now, surrender to Kṛṣṇa without hesitation.  
We said, “No, no, no! We’ve no qualification.”  
You said, “Yes, mleccha is your classification.  
But Gaurāṅga’s mercy has no limitation.

“And when you go preaching, just tell people, ‘Look,  
Just see how Lord Kṛṣṇa has come as a book!’  
Distribute books everywhere, by hook or by crook!”  
We took up our book-bags, and all the world shook.

Then Kṛṣṇa decided to take you away.  
(Was it five years ago? It just seems like five days.)  
“We’ll be helpless without you, “ was all we could say.  
You said, “If you love me, then just cooperate. “

Now our only relief is remembering you,  
Reading Līlāmṛta, writing Vyāsa-pūjā.  
Forgive me for saying you are ISKCON’s glue.  
Please keep us together, we want to serve you.

*Your servants in ISKCON Baltimore,*

## Bangkok

**You Are the One**

Covered by lust, too blind to realize  
among us, who would dare to visualize  
our destination according to karma  
unthinkable, the most frightening drama

ŚRĪLA PRABHUPĀDA, YOU ARE THE ONE  
nobody could do what you have done  
You freely gave us this matchless gift  
drowning souls in the ocean of death adrift

Śrī Caitanya had promised the world a boon  
every town and village will hear His name soon  
Śrīla Bhaktivinoda predicted a person will appear  
to take the holy names to the Western sphere

ŚRĪLA PRABHUPĀDA, YOU ARE THE ONE  
just when Vedic culture was almost gone  
to fulfill these prophecies of disciplic succession  
in a world of agony, nations in aggression

Many so-called yogīs and saints had appeared  
hiding lust and greed behind a big beard,  
to juggle words with a fake smile on the face  
injecting the poison of impersonal ways

ŚRĪLA PRABHUPĀDA, YOU ARE THE ONE  
Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta’s perfect son  
by bona fide śāstric explanations  
wearing Viṣṇu tilaka and kāṇṭhi-mālā  
exposed the heart of those “incarnations”  
as that of a riksha-wala

Godless scientists were proud to declare  
God is dead or has never been there  
by using modern media and electronic ware  
a person to even doubt them, extremely rare

ŚRĪLA PRABHUPĀDA, YOU ARE THE ONE  
accepting their challenge, sovereignly won  
presenting the Vedic conclusion flawless, clear  
using their own weapons, the latest gear  
to publish books, millions been sold  
preaching expertly, dedicated and bold  
Even if there were a few  
their ideas vague or a concocted brew  
claiming to believe in God or so  
nature is god or some eternal glow

ŚRĪLA PRABHUPĀDA, YOU ARE THE ONE  
it’s you we are fully depending on  
who placed Lord Jagannātha on the San Francisco throne  
teaching that the Deity is not wood or stone  
only by your unalloyed love and devotion  
Kṛṣṇa crossed the Atlantic and Pacific Ocean  
allowing Himself to be crated, shipped and cleared  
to please you, our conception was weird  
thus Deity forms of the Lord with splendid faces  
made Their appearance in various places  
Rukmiṇī-Dvārakādhiśa, Rādhā-Gokulānanda  
Kiśora-Kiśorī, Rādhā-Vṛndāvanacandra  
Rādhā-Govinda to name a few  
to Westerners entirely new  
These forms with gorgeous features  
are the source of life for serious preachers  
They manifested on  
Their own account, that’s for sure  
conquered by your genuine love, spotless and pure

A dynamic worldwide operation  
vigorously spreading, no limitation  
in this movement preaching is the essence  
where is that fool to deny your presence?

ŚRĪLA PRABHUPĀDA, YOU ARE THE ONE  
without your guidance it couldn’t go on

it’s a hellish brain which thinks you are gone  
For instance, standing at the printing machine  
watching a new book come out neat and clean  
your words in a new language, printed never before  
very exciting, nothing could satisfy the self more  
And your presence can intensely be perceived  
even by those who had never believed  
our service may take so many ways  
but it is always the energy of Your Divine Grace.

*The devotees at ISKCON Bangkok /Thailand*

## Bangladesh

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to your lotus feet!

You have given us

Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi  
Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda  
Śrī Sr-i Rādhā-Mādhava  
Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Vṛndāvanacandra  
Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gokulānanda  
Śrī Śrī Śrī Jagannātha-Subhadrā-Baladeva and so many other worshipable forms of the Lord.

You have given us the most precious gift, pure devotional service to the holy name of the Lord.

You went amongst the *yavanas* and *mlecchas,* preached, and thus, out of the most unlikely candidates, created *sādhu-saṅga* for us: the International Society for Krishna Consciousness.

You have given us Māyāpur, Vṛndāvana, Ratha-yātrā, temples, *prasādam, saṅkīrtana,* farms, *tilaka, guru-kula* and transcendental art.

You have given us BBT (your heart), ISKCON (your body) and *lakṣmī* (your blood).

Your gifts are innumerable and still expanding.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for these wonderful transcendental gifts. Thank you for your eternal protection.

*From your disciples and granddisciples in Bangladesh*

P.S. We also have a request: now please shed a special glance of mercy on this unfortunate land, once a bastion of Vaiṣṇavism, now covered by lamentation. Wherever your message is broadcast here, immediately people become joyful; but the darkness is great, and we are but few, and weak.

## Belo Horizonte

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracārine*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, as the perfect *ācārya* you came to reveal the most intimate relationships with the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, as Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu Himself did five hundred years ago. As a perfect *rūpānuga,* your perfect message was never polluted by any tinge of *jñāna* and *karma,* but it shows the highest platform of *prema-bhakti.*

O Gurudeva, on this most auspicious day let me understand more your unalloyed and exalted position as a *mahā-bhāgavata,* the bona fide representative of the disciplic succession from Lord Caitanya.

Only if I have the sublime vision of your supreme position will I not become a *guru-tyāgī,* or one who gives up the order of the *guru. Māyā* is extremely dangerous, and only those who are completely attached to your lotus feet, O Gurudeva, can distinguish what is what.

O Gurudeva, since you left, the world is not the same! As Śrīmatī Kuntīdevī, lamenting, said that the earth wouldn’t be the same after Kṛṣṇa’s departure, similarly we are all crying, lamenting for our bad fortune and for that of humanity. But you left us with a matchless gift—your unalloyed children, who, like yourself, are making this movement a big fight against *māyā.*

Therefore, Śrīla Prabhupāda, this auspicious appearance of yours in this hellish world of Kali-yuga is like refreshing water in a barbarian, corrupted and scorching social system. Your words will lead society for thousands of years. That was the purpose of your appearance.

O Gurudeva, the earth now is being protected again by your reestablishment of *bhāgavata-dharma!* You came and formed this big ISKCON, which is the body of the Lord Himself.

O Gurudeva, you were sent by Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu to form this movement and then reveal it to humanity! You boldly assured every one of us that this is the perfect system for the benefit of all.

O brilliant light of this dark and strange age! Please protect me always, accepting me as your most insignificant servant.

## Benelux

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances. All glories to Śrī Guru and Śrī Gaurāṅga.

Upon examining the history of Kali-yuga, regarded today with undue reverence by dint of its mistaken “advancements,” we may quickly observe that your position within this history is unparalleled.

Undoubtedly, history has produced many great personalities, both by spiritual and material standards. Such historical greatness is generally measured by the way and to what degree a person has been able to influence his environment and the history succeeding him. Another important consideration is into what area of society the influence of such a great person has been extended. History has known great personalities in the field of religion, speculative philosophy, science, technology, politics and art. That same history has revealed, though, that religious preceptors excel all others in influence. Religious teachings have demonstrated a remarkable ability to survive the ravages of time and human nature. Through the ages, religious teachings have thus provided human society with a substratum of serenity in which it can take refuge during times of reflection, contemplation or disillusionment with material objectives. Moreover, it can be said that religion is the fountainhead of civilization, even in its purely material aspects. As such, religion is the cradle of human life and distinguishes man from animal.

Certainly, history has produced many great religious teachers, mystics and theistic philosophers. All of them have had a greater or lesser influence on the course of history, and many of their teachings are still followed today. By dint of Vedic knowledge, it is known that within this period of what is called history, amongst the succession of many great religious teachers, two especially empowered religious teachers appeared who were incarnations of the Supreme Personality of Godhead. The first was Lord Buddha, who significantly altered the course of history by preaching nonviolence and, for esoterical reasons that are not always understood, agnosticism. Lord Buddha shook the foundations of a society called the Vedic culture, of which modern man knows little, but which society is the common ancestor and source of today’s civilizations. By the vicissitude of destiny and the destructive force of time, the influence of Kali-yuga had gradually infected the foundations of that great Vedic culture and gradually deprived it of its virtues and glories. Lord Buddha had succeeded at least in restoring a sense of morality, however, at the cost of theism.

The second incarnation was Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, who came to destroy agnosticism and spread love of God. Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s position is hard to understand; no *avatāra* ever before revealed the total absolute truth inasmuch as Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu did. Not merely does His metaphysical doctrine of *acintya-bhedābheda* surpass all other theistic doctrines, but more important, He freely distributed to anyone, including the most fallen, the highest emotion a living entity is capable of experiencing.

Still, in one sense, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have even excelled the greatness of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu restricted His preaching to India, but you have spread the *saṅkīrtana* movement all over the world. Sometimes the Supreme Personality of Godhead., in order to glorify His pure devotee, arranges events in such a way that His devotee may take the credit for work the Lord could have executed Himself. One time you were presented with an argument that was often voiced by envious people who were attempting to minimize your achievements and discredit your personality. The argument implied that if Śrī Caitanya wanted the *saṅkīrtana* movement to be spread to the West, why had He not gone there Himself (the meaning implied obviously being that Mahāprabhu was actually not interested in spreading Vaiṣṇavism all over the world, and that as such, anyone’s endeavors to do so did not entail any special merit)? When this argument was presented to you on a morning walk, you stopped, put your cane down firmly, and spoke, “Caitanya Mahāprabhu did not go to the West because He wanted to give me the credit.”

It is a fact that your achievement, in terms of establishing cells of pure Vaiṣṇavism in every part of the world, thus laying the foundation for a new world order based on Vedic principles, is an act of such historical magnitude that it baffles our attempts to comprehend it. No other event, with its consequent effects on history, can equal the fundamental and revolutionary changes that your work and teachings will bring about in a not-so-distant future. They will shake the foundation of civilization all over the world and will change the meaning of the word itself.

In trying to define your position from a historical perspective it seems that its special significance is derived from the fact that you have combined the highest scientific knowledge of the Absolute Truth with the highest degree of devotion and purity, and you have distributed them to everyone by using all means to your avail, not shunning the dubious advantages of Kali-yuga technology. It is interesting to note that of all technological advances, those in the field of transportation and communication have been the most substantial. It is exactly in those areas that technology has been most useful to the cause of spreading Vaiṣṇavism, and sometimes it even appears that it was specially created by Kṛṣṇa to make it possible for you to establish the Hare Kṛṣṇa movement all over the world within ten years. You made a remark at one time how the hippie movement was just created by Kṛṣṇa so that Kṛṣṇa consciousness could be established in the West. Thinking along these lines it does not seem unreasonable to postulate the idea that history has molded itself around your appearance. Your appearance and preaching activities may then be seen as the culmination of an evolutionary historical process, which will in the ultimate issue bring about a total spiritual revolution in order to change that history and with it the fate of billions of conditioned living entities. From this it seems that our conclusion in regards to your position in history as being unparalleled is justified. We, your disciples, are amongst those billions of conditioned living entities whose fate has been and will be changed due to your intervention. We can feel the bliss of serving you and assisting you in bringing about these historical changes, so that the good fortune that has befallen us may be shared by everyone, for thousands of years to come.

For this we are very grateful to you and eternally indebted, and with us, history will show, thousands of generations in the future.

*Your disciples and granddisciples from the Benelux Yātrā,*

## Bhaktivedanta Ashram (Sydney)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your feet.

On this your auspicious appearance day, we at Bhaktivedanta Ashram would like to thank you very much for appearing in this material world.

Your qualities shine forth just like the sunrise at dawn. Before meeting you we thought that such-and-such a person had good qualities, or such-and-such a thing was nice, but when the sun of Kṛṣṇa consciousness rises, upon meeting your person, one can see that all such material concepts are simply the wet and dry side of stool.

Transcendental qualities go far beyond the material concepts of good and bad. Your qualities are all unlimited and pure. You are always gentle and kind but still expert and grave; as harsh as a thunderbolt, yet as soft as a flower. Showing all the traits of a transcendental person, you blessed us with your presence and made our lives auspicious.

Now the only meaning to our lives is to serve you. Take away our service to your good self, and we are simply left with zero.

*Your servants,*
