## Bhaktivedanta Manor

On this occasion of the appearance of His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, let us remember him, very carefully recollecting whatever we may know and realize about his life and teaching. We therefore pray to Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu for His mercy so that we can write something a little beneficial for the hearing of the members of the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement, which was so painstakingly established by our beloved Gurudeva.

Śrīla Prabhupāda is actually so great, and his qualities so numerous, that we are not able to speak to any extent about these things. We can clearly see, however, that his whole life was completely dedicated to untiring, unmotivated, unflinching service at the feet of his Guru Mahārāja, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura. There is no one else who is remotely near to Śrīla Prabhupāda’s standard of service to Lord Kṛṣṇa. As it is clearly stated in *śāstra*,

*sa veda dhātuḥ padavīṁ parasya*  
*duranta-vīryasya rathāṅga-pāṇeḥ*  
*yo ’māyayā santatayānuvṛttyā*  
*bhajeta tat-pāda-saroja-gandham*

**“Only those who render unreserved, uninterrupted, favorable service unto the lotus feet of Lord Kṛṣṇa, who carried the wheel of the chariot in His hand, can know the creator of the universe in His full glory, power, and transcendence.” (*****Bhāg*****. 1.3.38)**

It is obvious that Śrīla Prabhupāda alone is able to enter into the most confidential regions of the Lord’s mind, and thus he is able to deliver Kṛṣṇa to us. He had unflinching faith in his Guru Mahārāja, while others did not.

The offering of Vyāsa-pūjā, our daily worship, the *sat-saṅga* of our Godbrothers, our study of Śrīla Prabhupāda’s transcendental books, and our service to his mission all focus our mind on the unique glories of our spiritual master and deepen our faith in him. From such fixed faith in the *guru*, our spiritual life blossoms. Any influence from any source is poison if it acts to reduce even fractionally our appreciation of Śrīla Prabhupāda as the representative of the *paramparā*, who alone possessed the full measure of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta’s mercy and who cared enough and loved us so intensely that he sacrificed everything for our benefit. Thus only some ungrateful, renegade disciple could conceive of comparing the service of a contemporary with that of Śrīla Prabhupāda.

These facts must be clearly understood in order to place our unalloyed trust in his lotus feet, so that there is some hope of this same standard being perpetuated. We cannot afford to water down this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement at the expense of the spiritual perfection of millions and millions of souls in the future.

A genuine disciple does not have a different opinion from his spiritual master about anything. Only a scoundrel will attempt to replace the order of the guru with feelings of his own heart or conscience. The command is there: *ājñā gurūṇāṁ hy avicāraṇīyā*. Follow the order of guru! Give up all personal consideration!

When speaking at Bhaktivedanta Manor in 1973 on the meaning of Vyāsa-pūjā, Śrīla Prabhupāda told us that it is very easy to be a spiritual master—all you have to do is follow your own spiritual master. But if you become overintelligent and add or change one word of the message that is being passed down, everything will be spoiled.

Are we honestly prepared to ruin Śrīla Prabhupāda’s movement because we are too proud to surrender to his basic instructions? Work cooperatively together under the GBC! Śrīla Prabhupāda taught us time and time again to follow his authorized representative, even if that person or body may appear, in our opinion, to have made a mistake. That is our authorization; otherwise we are no longer authorized ourselves. If we cannot cooperate together in a real mood of surrender, love, and trust, then it would have been better for us to have died long ago, when Śrīla Prabhupāda left our mortal vision.

We should understand that it’s the guru’s duty to chastise his disciple. That is his humility; not that he gets pleasure from always criticizing us. Only thinking of our welfare, the guru releases us from the shackles of false ego. We shouldn’t run to the shelter of another, who will simply decorate us with false prestige, and comfort our so-called wounded heart—“He who praises me is my enemy”—with no good to be gained, but rather great harm. Prabhupāda never said that we are the “personification of the *Bhāgavatam*” or “*parama*-Vaiṣṇavas.” He was never so cruel as to let us become falsely proud and so fall into the pit of illusion, thinking that we are anything but dogs, rascals, and fools. Real humility is shown by how one reacts to the chastisement of the spiritual master, not by some external show.

A disciple who is genuinely trying to surrender to his guru will always feel encouraged, whether he is chastised, neglected, praised, or apparently mistreated. Prabhupāda used to say that sincerity of purpose is the key. Lord Rāmacandra recognized that everything belonged to the *brāhmaṇas* and He simply ruled out of duty, quite prepared to step down from His post if there was even the slightest trace of a question about the authenticity of His position. We may work very hard and may acquire temples, money, disciples, fame, etc., but everything belongs to Śrīla Prabhupāda; nothing is ours.

If I leave the shelter of the paramparā by neglecting the instructions of Śrīla Prabhupāda, my spiritual progress is halted; and if I commit offenses to the lotus feet of my guru by attacking the mission carefully nurtured by his authorized representatives, my previous spiritual assets are destroyed; and if I then claim proprietorship over Prabhupāda’s temples, money and granddisciples, I am public enemy number one of my spiritual master.

For pure Vaiṣṇavism, the influences of Māyāvāda and *sahajiyā* are most dangerous, and here in England, as we write this offering, we are feeling the attack of *māyās* forces in such a cruel and subtle form.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are struggling with our own spiritual practices, what to speak of the responsibility of managing your movement still in its fledgling stage. We are therefore praying to all the Vaiṣṇavas in ISKCON that we may remember you at every step of our lives and continue serving you in a pure spirit of doing the needful at all times. And we are helplessly appealing to Lord Nṛsiṁha-deva for the great honor of being able to sacrifice any amount of energy for the protection and nourishment of the institution you began for saving the lost souls of Kali-yuga.

*We beg to remain eternally your servants,*

## Bhaktivedanta Swami Gurukula

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please allow us to humbly submit that we are totally useless from every angle of vision. It is only by your causeless mercy that we are able to take pen in hand and make some feeble attempt to estimate your unlimited greatness and glory. Although we are neither scholarly nor poetic in any sense, kindly permit us now to somehow render an appreciation, echoing the sentiments of the realized souls. For alas, walking in the treads of their lives, we leave no tracks. Helplessly bobbing in their wake, we make no waves. We are lame, but well-advised by your divinely inspired instructions.

*tapyante loka-tāpena*  
*sādhavaḥ prāyaśo janāḥ*  
*paramārādhanaṁ tad dhi*  
*puruṣasyākhilātmanaḥ*

**“It is said that great personalities almost always accept voluntary suffering because of the suffering of people in general. This is considered the highest method of worshiping the Supreme Personality of Godhead, who is present in everyone’s heart.” (*****Bhāg*****. 8.7.44)**

In the Eighth Canto of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*,you have so kindly described how Lord Śiva, the greatest of all Vaiṣṇavas, shrank the ocean of poison to an amount which fit the palm of his hand and then drank the deadly mixture, thus saving the entire universe. In the purport, you explain that the *parama-ārādhanam*, or highest worship of the Supreme Lord, is to benedict all conditioned souls with Kṛṣṇa consciousness. This you have surely done and will continue to do millions of times over. You are completely desireless save and except for propagating the Lord’s mission. By your divine wisdom and unflinching love for Kṛṣṇa, you have so mercifully shrunk the poisonous ocean of Kali and held back the devastating tidal waves of passion and ignorance which would otherwise annihilate whatever good qualities remain.

But the most amazing thing, at least to our eyes, is the great personal expense and inconvenience which you undertook on our behalf. And what is our qualification to receive such respite? Simply your causeless mercy. Who can imagine? Who can understand? Who can even begin to appreciate the vastness and depth of your transcendental vision, your supreme compassion? In this blistered age of trouble, when the hatetorn world is tottering on the brink of oblivion, a ray of hope has entered the hearts of billions who would otherwise wallow in a morass of self-despair.

You are the brain, Śrīla Prabhupāda. We are simply the limbs of your body. Please guide us in such a way that we may somehow become instrumental in fulfilling the order of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. Give us strength. Protect us from the relentless onslaught of *māyā* which pounds mercilessly upon our battered consciousness. Please enable us to serve you in a manner befitting the most confidential servitor of the Lord, one whose opulences can never be fully expounded even by millions of tongues. Such is your greatness—eternal and inexhaustible.

In the *Bhagavad-gītā,* Eleventh Chapter, verse 44, Arjuna says to Kṛṣṇa,

*tasmāt praṇamya praṇidhāya kāyaṁ*  
*prasādaye tvām aham īśam īḍyam*  
*piteva putrasya sakheva sakhyuḥ*  
*priyaḥ priyāyārhasi deva soḍhum*

**“You are the Supreme Lord, to be worshiped by every living being. Thus I fall down to offer You my respects and ask Your mercy. Please tolerate the wrongs that I may have done to You and bear with me as a father with his son, or a friend with his friend, or a lover with his beloved.”**

You explain in the purport that Kṛṣṇa’s devotees relate to Him in various relationships, such as son, husband, friend, master, etc. As the father, husband or master tolerates, so Kṛṣṇa tolerates. It is in this mood, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that we are requesting you to somehow tolerate our madness, our misgivings, our bunglings, our offenses, and to please bear with us so that we may become empowered to spread your fame and that of the Supreme Lord all over the world.

*Your servants,*

Dhanurdhāra Swami / Rūpa-vilāsa dāsa adhikārī / Dayānanda dāsa adhikārī / Nayanābhirāma dāsa adhikārī / Karṇāmrta dāsa adhikārī / Raghunātha dāsa brahmacārī / Pūrṇacandra dāsa brahmacārī / Yajña dāsa brahmacārī / Ananta-rūpa dāsa brahmacārī / Candrikā-devī dāsī / Nandarāṇī-devī dāsī

## Bhubaneswar

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On this glorious day of your appearance we offer our humble obeisances at your lotus feet again and again, because you have blessed us with your causeless mercy, giving us secure shelter at your lotus feet. We have no words to express your mercy. How merciful you are!

When we were suffering in the burning fire of material existence you came to deliver us, out of your compassion. As stated in the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* (3.5.3):

*janasya kṛṣṇād vimukhasya daivād*  
*adharma-śīlasya suduḥkhitasya*  
*anugrahāyeha caranti nūnaṁ*  
*bhūtāni bhavyāni janārdanasya*

**“O my Lord, great philanthropic souls travel on the earth on behalf of the Supreme Personality of Godhead to show compassion on the fallen souls who are averse to the sense of subordination to the Lord.”**

So you came out of compassion for fallen souls like us and traveled all over the world with the mission of bringing souls back to Godhead, back home. It is a great *mahātmās* or Vaiṣṇava’s duty. You are such a great Vaiṣṇava.

We had forgotten Kṛṣṇa, and you taught us how to become Kṛṣṇa conscious and serve Kṛṣṇa. We are very wicked fellows. We have so many lusty desires. But you saved us; you made us taste the nectar of *kṛṣṇa-prema. How* nice is the taste of that nectar you have given us! We have nothing to pay you back with, and we cannot pay you back. Please shower your causeless mercy upon us that we will be able to fulfill your desire and serve you eternally.

As Śrīla Bhaktivinoda sings, *yogyatā vicāre kichu nāi pāi, tomāra karuṇā sāra.* We have no qualification; only your mercy is required. Really, if you just shower your causeless mercy on us, we will be able to fulfill your desire here in Bhubaneswar. Otherwise it is not possible.

You have the desire to build a nice Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma temple and establish a famous ISKCON center here. And you have also the desire of printing your books in Oriya.

But we are such unfortunate and unworthy persons here that we have not yet done anything tangible or spectacular to fulfill your desire and make you happy. We have just made a humble beginning. Please make us your fully surrendered servants to fulfill your mission here in Bhubaneswar by making us your instruments- instruments in thy hands. Moreover, we humbly pray on this auspicious day, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that you give us strength to realize your mercy and appreciate your benediction more and more.

*Your humble servants in ISKCON Bhubaneswar,*

Gaura-Govinda Swami / Tejas dāsa / Vaiṣṇava dāsa / Sādhu-hṛdaya dāsa / Brahma-hari dāsa / Sādhu dāsa / Kṣīrodakaśāyī dāsa / Bhakta Nanda Kiśora / Bhakta Ajaya / Mādirā-devī dāsī / Tejasvinī

## Bombay

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our fallen obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

*janma karma ca me divyam*  
*evaṁ yo vetti tattvataḥ*  
*tyaktvā dehaṁ punar janma*  
*naiti mām eti so ’rjuna*

**“One who knows the transcendental nature of My appearance and activities does not, upon leaving the body, take his birth again in this material world, but attains to My eternal abode, O Arjuna.”**

You have taken a so-called birth in this material world, not because you are unable to understand Kṛṣṇa’s activities, like so many of us, but rather, you appeared in Gauḍa-deśa just to execute the mission of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. Thus Gauḍa-deśa is worshipable not only because Lord Caitanya appeared there, but also because He arranged for you to appear in the same worshipable place. The Lord predicted that His name would be sung in every town and village of the world, and so also the great devotee of the Lord Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura predicted that the time would come when European and Indian devotees would be chanting the holy name together in the streets of Navadvīpa. To keep the prediction of His great devotee, Lord Gaurāṅga chose you to propagate His name. Once you were asked why Lord Caitanya preached only in Bhārata-varṣa, and you raised your cane and firmly planted it with ironlike conviction and replied, “Because Kṛṣṇa wants *me* to get the credit \[for preaching Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world\]!” As the *śāstra* teaches, the devotee of the Lord is even more merciful than the Lord Himself. So benevolently have you accepted us fools and rascals and disciplined us to propagate this Caitanya cult, just as Lord Rāmacandra accepted so many monkey devotees and engaged them to build a wondrous bridge across the ocean. Please give us your blessings to assist you to bridge the gap from the spiritual world and kill the Rāvaṇa mentality with preaching. You have already carefully trained us for this purpose. You are like a touchstone. Your glance alone has transformed so many stone hearts into melted gold. In your last days in Vṛndāvana, you were wondering how this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement would go on without you. Actually, the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement can never go on without you, but as you always said, “I shall live forever in my books,” and you also live in your instructions and training.

As devotees of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Rāsavihārījī, we are fully aware that you wanted to build a glorious temple on Hare Kṛṣṇa Land, Juhu, even before leaving for America. When you passed the then Nair Wadi on your way to Smt. Sumati Morarji’s bungalow, before leaving India, you saw a vacant field and thought, “What a nice place to build a temple for Kṛṣṇa!” And Kṛṣṇa always fulfills the desires of His pure devotees. As Tamāla Kṛṣṇa Goswami remarked, “I have often heard of devotees undergoing austerities to please the Lord, but at Hare Kṛṣṇa Land, Rādhā-Rāsavihārījī underwent austerities to please Their pure devotee.” Their Lordships stood for years in Their shed, just to fulfill your transcendental desire.

We beg that we may forever assist you in your service to Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Rāsavihārījī. You said that Bombay is your office. Let us beat the *bṛhad-mṛdaṅga* from your office by publishing more books, making more devotees, establishing more centers and creating more farms, and by broadcasting your glories more and more—glorying Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Rāsavihārījī more and more and more and more. We beg to remain,

*Gopī-bhartuḥ pada-kamalayor dāsa-dāsānudāsaḥ,*  
*Your disciples and granddisciples,*

## Boston

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracārine*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

O Prabhupāda, just see what you have done!  
You’ve opened the door to freedom to everyone!  
Freedom from lamentation, illusion and fear,  
and all we have to do is simply hear—  
hear from the lips of the pure devotee.  
Listen! What is he saying? He has the key  
that can set you free from misery  
and give you full knowledge, bliss and eternity.  
Wake up jīva! Wake up jīva! Prabhupāda’s books can save you.  
Be a little intelligent, be a little reflective. Just see what  
you’ve been through.  
Morose, morose, your life is really gross.  
Illusion, confusion, ignorance in profusion.  
You have to die, you want to know why.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda can give you that spiritual eye.  
O doomed creature down in the well,  
why take a tour of the planets of hell?  
Look up! Grab hold of Prabhupāda’s mercy-rope;  
Śrīla Prabhupāda’s ISKCON is your only hope.  
The glowworm speculators don’t have any remedy  
to save the plummeting sub-śūdra society.  
But you, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, can solve the disparity  
with your transcendental words of crystal clarity.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, your books propound the highest truth,  
how to render loving devotional service to that ever-fresh youth,  
Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Absolute Truth.  
There is a way out of our hopelessness:  
dear Prabhupāda, you gave us Kṛṣṇa consciousness.  
To glorify you we know we are truly unfit,  
for you are the master at whose feet all masters sit.  
We know you are very, very dear to God,  
therefore we sing, “All glories to our beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda!”

*Your servants at Boston, ISKCON,*

## Brazil Farm

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Today the auspicious day of your appearance on this planet is celebrated. Today the demigods, the great *ṛṣis*, Gandharvas, Kinnaras and Apsarās should get together to glorify you in chorus, but even then their prayers would be insufficient.

As no one can light the sun nor moisten the ocean, no one can increase your glory, because you are glorious in yourself. However, in trying to glorify you, it is the glorifier who becomes glorified, just as a decoration on Kṛṣṇa’s body becomes decorated. Such is the nature of your renunciation and greatness.

Prabhupāda, you kept the message of Śrī Caitanya carefully within yourself. You kept it purely, just as you understood it with your transcendental intelligence through the lotus lips of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are without any desire of gain or personal honor; full freedom from fear, false anxiety and lust; pleased with what comes of its own accord, seeing Kṛṣṇa’s hands behind everything; humble and simple; always enthusiastic, patient, trustworthy and fully surrendered to your *guru*;preaching constantly all over the world and anywhere the science of Kṛṣṇa consciousness—these are only some of the infinite qualities with which you presented the message that would save the world.

When Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta passed away, the real faces were seen. In the absence of the *guru*, the preaching died in the enthusiasm of your Godbrothers. But not in yours, because from the very beginning you made of your *guru’s* order your life and soul. “Our master asked us to preach in every town and village, as predicted and desired by Lord Gaurāṅga.” This was your clear understanding; this is what you taught us. But your Godbrothers did not understand that, and thus you became an orphan.

You were alone when you left the country on board the *Jaladuta* to come and save us. Surely you came with a strong determination to preach, to preach openly and boldly this message we all needed so much. This was your law and your order. No law would work besides this supreme one. The caste *brāhmaṇas* could complain for their position in the caste system. They could dare to say that to cross the ocean is an offense, as well as giving a sacred thread to a *mleccha* or preaching *vāsudeva-lī1ā* in the country of the barbarians. But all their complaints were ended by the light of knowledge that you would show them.

You took the truth wherever you went, and who can stop the truth that is chanted by thousands of verses in the *Vedas*? You gave the truth with your firm and sweet voice. The *mlecchas* also became disturbed, because they deny His existence or they may say God is a speechless energy. In this way scientists and politicians, so-called religious leaders and philosophers, all were killed like moths in the fire of your strong arguments. Your lotus tongue smashed their position and mundane theories with the strength of Paraśurāma’s ax. So you were always victorious, more and more glorious day by day. And that you may be forever, not because we are showing you as glorious, but because in your mercy you are revealing yourself more to the world.

You left everything to come and save us. You surrendered completely to the mission of giving pure love for the Supreme Personality of Godhead. How can we repay you our debt? Even if we surrendered completely unto your lotus feet, with our body, mind, devotion and intelligence, it would still be a useless payment. But what can we do besides that? Still, we have to beg for your mercy to have the strength to surrender unto you in this way.

Therefore, Śrīla Prabhupāda, with folded hands and in all humility we beg you to please make us like bees who go to the lotus flower to take its honey and become drunk in its sweetness, never able to leave it. Finally, when the dusk comes, the lotus closes its petals, arresting the bee which is inside. Similarly, let us become this much attached to the transcendental sweetness of your lotus feet for eternity. And by the time the sun of our Kṛṣṇa consciousness is setting and *māyā* starts showing us all its inconveniences and material fear, then at this time, please close the lotus petals of your lotus feet and trap us forever, so that until our last breath we live only for you.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you boldly preached throughout the world, not finding anyone who could really challenge you. Nobody was able to stop your enlightening preaching. As your children, please order us to go and preach all over the world. Do not allow this sacred fire you lit with so much love extinguish within our whimsical hearts. Please, send us to preach this wonderful message throughout the world. Let us satisfy your desire by always being under the shelter of your sacred lotus feet.

*Your fallen children at Nova Gokula Farm,*

## Caitanya College

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet.

Vyāsa-pūjā day is a day of worship. This day we (your spiritual children and grandchildren) offer you our homage as the representative of Vyāsadeva and the Supreme Personality of Godhead. Composing this offering we consider so many appropriate lines and *ślokas* to glorify Your Divine Grace. Certainly they are sincere and suitable at this time. But in our hearts, we question that this is the substance of your worship on this occasion.

In your 1961 Vyāsa-pūjā offering you chided your Godbrothers, saying, “a festival of fruits and flowers does not constitute worship. The one who serves the message of the *guru* really worships him …” So this celebration should be a pronouncement of offerings that shows how we have served your mission throughout the year. For while words may be easily composed, the evidence of service is substantial and indicative of true love and devotion. So we could make attempts at saying how many books we distributed last year, or how many new devotees were made—surely there were some substantial advances on various preaching fronts: life membership, public relations, dean of Westminster, and so on. But it seems that for all these hard efforts and glorious conquests, the current events are so grave and catastrophic that they dwarf our past gains. You are always present throughout our lives, witnessing our efforts, Śrīla Prabhupāda. And even more so now than ever, on this day when everyone’s consciousness is unilaterally focused on you. As you would ask your disciples at the end of their preaching day, “So, what is the report?” so we feel dishonest if we omit the disastrous events of the year.

As your loving disciples we only want to offer you wonderful things—a world conquered by the holy name, your books in each and every home, and Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa Deities as the focus of everyone’s life. We want to see your loving smiles and your nectarean blessings, affirming that you are pleased. But the events speak for themselves. A great violence, great harm, has been done to your International Society for Krishna Consciousness, to your very body and very self. You have told us that you are nondifferent from ISKCON—a transcendental organization of Vaiṣṇavas that has been established to fulfill the desire of our disciplic succession dating back five thousand years to spread the holy name to every tongue all over the world. And to establish this society you prepared for seventy years and then struggled alone, against inconceivable obstacles in the darkest era of the material world. Then you guided, protected and cultivated ISKCON, and when you were satisfied, you left it to continue to fulfill the mission of Caitanya Mahāprabhu. You had said that “History will note how this ISKCON society has saved the world.”

To attack or disturb ISKCON in its fledgling years after your departure is an unspeakable, unthinkable offense to you. While ISKCON is impervious to external attacks, it is affected by internal disorders.

And we have been witnesses, bystanders, while your movement in the United Kingdom has been hacked to pieces, and we cannot help but feet that perhaps we, too, are guilty through negligence. Knowingly, through ignorance, or accidentally, do we not share some of the blame for this catastrophe? Twelve years of arduous effort and personal involvement by you has been viciously and selfishly attacked. (Is the burden of responsibility just on the attacker’s side?) A fallen *ācārya*-GBC man, a hundred misled disciples, shattered spiritual lives, and a splinter group breaking from ISKCON. It would be easier to cry and run away and hide than to face you with such news, as your instructions echo in our ears: “Your love will be shown for me by how you cooperate together to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness.” Were we too patient? Should the issue have been forced sooner? Could a hundred souls have been saved and this zone spared the suffering?

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are your hapless dependents; other than you, we do not know anything. In our childish efforts to serve you, a weed grew alongside our creepers, and later that weed became the source of great turmoil. It is not some remote issue that happened somewhere, but a subjective manifestation of our own deficiencies.

The preaching has been hampered, but not destroyed, Prabhupāda. Things have not come to an end.

In your presence, sometimes devotees would commit mistakes, and they would come to offer their regrets with folded hands. But you would not be satisfied by apologies alone; rather, you demanded to know what solution would follow to rectify the fault. So our homage is to make this promise to you. Although many obstructions have arisen and many preaching tools hampered, we shall accept that burden as our service. We shall construct things as they were before, at any cost, personal consideration and inconvenience, neglecting all hardships. Śrīla Prabhupāda, we promise that we will reestablish a zone purer, more Kṛṣṇa conscious, with better Deity worship, more books distributed, more devotees made than before. We have only this promise, empty as it may seem, to offer you, but what other choice is there? We can simply ask you to wait and see our sincerity. The lesson has been well learned. To diverge even one atomic fragment from your instructions means disaster; therefore we etch in our hearts your instruction to “Just do as I do.” We are aspiring to be your servants, our offering is merely a promise, the present an embarrassment; we feel chastised, purified, but more committed to serving your mission than ever before. Please let us remain your eternal servants.

*The devotees of Caitanya College*

## Calcutta

**A Plea for Grace to Our Beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda**

Our days were bright when you were here,  
The clouds of ignorance had begun to clear,  
And though your presence is still in our midst,  
Māyā enwraps us in her deceptive mist.

For somehow our insincerity breeds  
a bewildering feeling of insecurity.

We tread without thought, fumble and stumble,  
Fallen and wounded, we constantly grumble.  
Motivated desires ward off your guiding voice,  
And our false ego prevents us from making the right choice.

When you were physically still at the helm,  
we felt we were in Kṛṣṇa’s realm.

Now we feel hapless and helpless, frightened and alone,  
Even though by your instructions you guide us safely home.  
A new danger confronts us as your wishes we forget,  
Bewildered by the forces you cautioned us against.

Perhaps this great struggle was wished by you,  
to teach us to be so sincere and true.

If only we surrendered completely to you  
and reached for your mercy, we’d know what to do.  
But we follow the dictates of our insatiable mind,  
Ruled by its whims and poisonous false pride.

Yet … we findfault outside ourselves,  
blaming circumstances and others and everything else.

Reflecting on our impurity, that completely overwhelms,  
Bewilders, and throws us in the darkest of realms.  
We pray for your mercy and saving grace.  
O Prabhupāda, save us from the problems we face!

Thus we pray in earnest to you,  
unwilling though we be, please help us and see us through.

For it is your grace that sustains us and leads us along,  
Your love that inspires us and keeps our convictions strong.  
May we always honor our commitments to you  
And learn to love Kṛṣṇa purely through you.

Submitted with immense gratitude by your love-bound disciples and granddisciples in Calcutta.

## Cape Town

**Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda**

Stuck in the mire of nescience deeper than  
the eye could see  
Lived a wretched little fellow.  
That little guy was me.  
Thinking myself to need happiness,  
Wanting to be really free,  
I made all kinds of plans  
But sunk deeper and deeper than deep.  
Moving about to be comfortable,  
Exercising my brain to the t,  
Just made life quite unbearable  
To continue in that filthy sea.  
A cry to help was quickly heard.  
“Here’s your chance to be free.  
So said a cloth-clad man.  
“But listen to me,”  
follow this advice and it will suffice,  
Free you from this devil’s paradise.  
Chant Hare Kṛṣṇa  
Chant Hare Rāma  
That was you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, with hari-nāma,  
Who lifted me out,  
Gave me fteedom from doubt.  
To follow Kṛṣṇa’s program—  
That’s your command  
For this age of the damned.  
Your glory—unknown  
to those of this abode  
was a challenge indeed,  
to sow all the seeds  
of bhakti-latā-bīja.  
Now the plants grow  
upwards, row on row,  
Searching for Kṛṣṇa’s lotus toes.  
You know the way,  
So we pray,  
Prabhupāda, stay with us one more day.

*Devotees of Cape Town,*

Kāla dāsa / Dhīrāṅga dāsa / Bhakta Freddy / Yaśomatī-devī dāsī / Rocanī-devī dāsī / Uṣā / Kṛṣṇadāsa / Sītā / Lalitā / Haridāsa / Marika / Bhaktin Allison

## Chicago

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracārine*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*bhūtānāṁ deva-caritaṁ*  
*duḥkhāya ca sukhāya ca*  
*sukhāyaiva hi sādhūnāṁ*  
*tvādṛśām acyutātmanām*

**“The activities of demigods lead to both misery and happiness for living beings, but the activities of great saints like you who have accepted the infallible Lord as their very soul result only in the happiness of all beings.” (*****Bhāg*****. 11.2.5)**

Throughout history there have been many so-called great persons who have come forward and performed many seemingly miraculous activities and feats. If we examine the activities of these persons, we will find their feats almost impossible for the common man to excel or even equal. Most men can only dream of achieving similar stature, a few may make some attempt, less succeed in equaling, and even fewer surpass their predecessors. But if we think about them honestly, what is the value of these achievements unless there is eternal benefit for all living entities? The performer may feel satisfied with his false pride, or enjoy some material facility as a result of this success, but these things are illusory and temporary, ending only in reflections of the past, misery and defeat.

Even if their achievements were beneficial for others, they are limited to the mundane welfare of their families, friends or country, and at the cost of great suffering for others. And then the benefits, being temporary, also end in suffering for the beneficiaries. So what is the real value of these so-called miraculous activities?

Śrīla Prabhupāda, today let us appreciate your divine appearance by remembering your transcendentally miraculous activities.

*bhagavan bhavato yātrā*  
*svastaye sarva-dehinām*  
*kṛpaṇānāṁ yathā pitror*  
*uttama-śloka-vartmanām*

**“My lord, your visit, like that of a father to his children, is for the benefit of all living beings. You especially help the most wretched among them, as well as those who are advanced on the path toward the Supreme Lord, Uttamaśloka.” (*****Bhāg*****. 11.2.4)**

Everything you did, Śrīla Prabhupāda, was for the ultimate benefit of all living entities, free from all motives for personal aggrandizement. In your activities you displayed unlimited compassion and tolerance, distributing freely the greatest gift, love of Kṛṣṇa, asking in return only that those who take this mercy distribute it freely. In fact, Prabhupāda, in order to take full advantage of your gift, one has to distribute and share it with others.

Prabhupāda, you have proven yourself as the most magnanimous of all persons by coming to the Western countries and delivering the most fallen of all persons and by making available to everyone the greatest of all secrets when you translated the *Bhagavad-gītā*, *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*, and *Caitanya-caritāmṛta*. You have exemplified these teachings of Lord Caitanya and perfectly carried out the order of your spiritual master, and in this way your very life is the greatest gift, completely perfect for the benefit of all living entities.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we only pray that we can please you by taking advantage of your mercy and by distributing profusely Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

*Your humble servants,*  
Chicago ISKCON

## Christchurch

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracārine*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Your glories are unlimited. If it were not for your coming to the West in 1965 to fulfill the order of Śrī Guru and Gaurāṅga, none of us would have been saved. The order of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu that all Indians would become perfect and then travel all over the world delivering others was not taken seriously by anyone before the appearance of Your Divine Grace. Even your Godbrothers admitted later that this instruction seemed too idealistic and that they were frightened to travel away from India, thinking they would fall down. But you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, as Abhaya Caraṇāravinda Bhaktivedanta Swami, a fearless devotee completely surrendered to Kṛṣṇa, came, unafraid, fully determined to fulfill your spiritual master’s desire. An advanced devotee is recognized by his preaching to the fallen conditioned souls. *Para-duḥkha-duḥkhī:* he is unhappy for the unhappiness of others. Even in your childhood you could not tolerate to see other living beings suffering. The movement you began is now enabling so many to become free of their miserable condition and experience the eternal life of full bliss and knowledge. On this most auspicious occasion of your appearance day we humbly beg for your blessings. The mercy we ask is that you kindly empower all of us to assist you in your mission of spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness, both here in New Zealand’s South Island and all over the universe.

Jagad-guru Śrīla Prabhupāda ki jaya!

*Your servants,*

Ālālanātha dāsa / Jagadātmā dāsa / Vanamālī dāsa / Dhanañjaya dāsa / Bhakta Bryce / Viṣṇupādī-devī dāsī / Śacimātā-devī dāsī / Pada-padma-śubha-śuci-devī dāsī / Candramukhī-devī dāsī / Rucirānana / Rūpa-mañjarī

## Cleveland

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracārine*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We are happy to be able to absorb our minds in remembrance of you through this Vyāsa-pūjā offering. We lament that Kṛṣṇa has sent you such incompetent servants like ourselves to assist you in such an enormous universal mission. We feel ourselves internally, externally and eternally indebted to you for accepting such pitiable servants. You have informed us that this *saṅkīrtana* movement is the benediction moon for the suffering humanity. And by accepting the shelter of your lotus feet, we are able to engage in the distribution of this love of Kṛṣṇa. Finding words to glorify or properly appreciate you is difficult for us, but we want to tell you that we love you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and we love your movement, your activities, your desires, your plans, your disciples, and your ISKCON. And we want to always be under your shelter by living to fulfill your desires only. We thank you for giving us the intelligence to enact your mission on earth, and to realize our uselessness trapped inside of these temporary lumps of mucus and bile and air. Your amazing ability and power to engage such fallen souls like ourselves is a living phenomenon that shocked our parents and countrymen. Everything that you did transgressed the limit of the mind’s ability to understand. Your actions were sometimes confusing, because they were not of this world, especially the tasks you undertook to publish the eighteen-thousand-verse *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* what to speak of the Bengali classic *Śrī Caitanya-caritāmṛta.* You have blessed the whole universe with nectar and the eternal fountain of youth. Your tireless preaching has uprooted deep desires for profit, adoration and distinction in the hearts of your fallen entourage. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have given us life, and with that life you also injected spiritual intelligence in the form of your causeless mercy. Without your selfless mercy we will immediately become void and useless. So therefore your mercy is all that we are made of. Being without your mercy is like fire without heat, or a body without a soul. So please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, please don’t cut off your mercy. Our bodies belong to you, so you can do what you want with us. But we are asking that you allow us to continue our service to you eternally. We want to distribute many, many books for you and benedict many conditioned souls. We know this will happen only by your mercy, so please empower us to fulfill your desire.

*sākṣād-dharitvena samasta-śāstrair*  
*uktas tathā bhāvyata eva sadbhiḥ*

You are as good as God, on account of being a bona fide representative, and you are to be honored and worshiped on an equal platform. Therefore, Śrīla Prabhupāda, make us dance for you; please make us surrender to you. Only then can we make progress on the path back to Godhead.

*kṛṣṇa se tomāra, kṛṣṇe dite pāro,*  
*tomāra śakati āche*  
*āmi to’ kāṅgāla, ‘kṛṣṇa’ ‘kṛṣṇa’ boli’,*  
*dhāi tava pāche pāche*

Kṛṣṇa is yours; you have the power to give Him to us. We are simply running behind you, shouting, “Kṛṣṇa! Kṛṣṇa!”

O great saintly person, Śrīla Prabhupāda, give us the shade of your lotus feet.

*We beg to remain your eternal servants at Cleveland Mandira*,

## Colombia

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracārine*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our prostrated obeisances at your lotus feet. On this auspicious day of your appearance we would like to thank you for permitting us to stay in line with all the other devotees to offer our homage to you. Though glorifying you is like showing with a candle the brilliance of the sun, we know that our words will not go in vain, because you have descended from Kṛṣṇaloka just to recover those nobody else could have recovered, to bring them back to Kṛṣṇa. The swan can extract the milk from a mixture of water and milk, but we will not find a swan in the dirty places. Therefore it was quite unexpected that a swanlike pure devotee like yourself would come to the most abominable part of the world to extract some desperate souls from this filthy mess and give them love of Kṛṣṇa. If we are allowed to stay under the shelter of the shade of your lotus feet, by your causeless mercy only, we want to promise in return that we will not stop distributing your books or living the simple life-style you taught us to live in the early ISKCON days. We feel separation from those days when you personally pushed us to go out to preach and not worry about our conveniences.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, without you as the center of our lives, this body is not worth being maintained. The years pass by, and still we cannot accomplish anything in comparison to your bold preaching. Please let our hearts become pure so that our preaching can become effective and we can bring new devotees to offer their *daṇḍavats* to your lotus feet. Then our hearts will feel happiness, and we can see within our minds your smiling face, your ever-smiling face. There is nothing else we want except pleasing you.

Remaining here we can only cry, Śrīla Prabhupāda. We are yours; please don’t leave us behind.

*Your eternal servants from ISKCON Colombia*

Bogota Mandira / Medellin Mandira / Kali Mandira / Rādhā Colombia Īśvara Mandira / Gurukula / Bucaramanga preaching center / Traveling Saṅkīrtana / ISEV Department for Vaiṣṇava Studies

## Dallas

**Master of Us All**

We were wandering,  
Wandering within this dark material world—  
Kali’s slaves.  
"Sworn to unclean work,  
Attracted by the impermanent.  
Perplexed by various anxieties  
And bound by a network of illusions."  
We were falling into hell.

You are light—  
A ray of Viṣṇu  
Coming by His causeless mercy  
To teach.  
To teach us that this dark, cold world is  
Duḥkhālayam aśāśvatam—  
A temporary place, full of misery.  
You opened up our eyes.

You are light—  
Kṛṣṇa’s dearmost servant  
Coming by your causeless mercy  
To preach.  
To preach about the glories of the Lord,  
Smashing our conceptions  
That all is void—  
You saved us ftom māyās last trap.

You were planting—  
Planting seeds of bhakti in our stonelike hearts.  
Mahāprabhu’s aide.  
Descended just to save us,  
Called us to awake,  
Brought us divine medicine  
And destroyed the disease of illusion.  
You have given us the greatest gift.

Prabhupāda, you are master—  
Lord and master of us all.  
How can we ever repay you?  
We can only pray to be your servants  
For birth after birth after birth.  
“O master! Be merciful to us.  
Give us the shade of your lotus feet.  
Your fame is spread all over the three worlds."

*Your eternal servants at Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kālacandjī Dhāma,*

## Denver

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracārine*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We do not presume to come before you presenting this offering as a product of our own spiritual prowess. Whatever we have, you have given us. Even as this offering is written, you sit within our hearts, our benevolent father, witnessing our fumbling, childlike attempts, yet still giving us enough insight to write a few words in praise of you, just so we may feel a little satisfied. That is your kindness upon us, because glorifying you and serving you is our life substance.

Of course, your glory is not in words alone—your glory is living, expanding and concrete. We, your devotees, are living testaments of your supreme benevolence and vast spiritual potencies. When you were once asked if it were possible for you to perform miracles, you replied that we are your miracles. Certainly this is true. You have taken the most wretched, bewildered persons and transformed our lives. You have done what no one else could have done—you have given us life, the real life of devotional service.

We owe you everything, yet how are we to repay you? The gift of Kṛṣṇa you have given has no equal. It is wholly impossible to repay the debt the disciple owes his spiritual master. Yet, as another feature of your kindness you have given us some indication. You once told us all, “Preach as I am preaching.” This is your formula for repaying our debt to you. Simultaneously this is our formula for glorifying you. As the preaching of the transcendental name and fame of the Supreme Lord, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, increases unlimitedly, your glories as His topmost, intimate servant and confidential devotee will simultaneously and automatically increase. This will be our satisfaction, the satisfaction of the Lord and the satisfaction of your ISKCON.

Therefore, let us preach together. Śrīla Prabhupāda, please empower us! Our words of praise alone are insufficient. Let our actions speak louder than these words. Please let your books rain from our hands “like bombs from the sky onto the laps of the conditioned souls. “ Let the *harināma* and the *prasādam* distribution increase unlimitedly. Let this be our offering to you and our solemn promise as well that we will never stop preaching as you preached, following your proven formulas, until our bodies cease to function. You have set the example, and we are prepared to follow you eternally. This alone can completely satisfy you and glorify you because you have given us Kṛṣṇa.

## Detroit

Most exalted Śrīla Prabhupāda,

After hours of meditation and contemplation, and the passage of many sober days in trying to write something in observance of the most auspicious day in the universe, your appearance day, we come to the same conclusion that we do every year when the same attempt is made: the conclusion that in the presence of your most revered and exalted personality, we are nothing but the most wretched and insignificant fools. As the minutes tick by, the pages are written and rewritten, and words, phrases, sentences and paragraphs are pulled out from the recesses of our hearts. Our best attempts are made to utilize all the resources and faculties we have available to perform the most demanding task that we are faced with—the task of being able to properly appreciate and glorify you.

Most astonishingly, however, all the intelligence, the wisdom, the knowledge, the ability, and our other resources that seem to be available throughout the year, lose all their potency. The *ślokas,* verses, prayers, and other words from your books seem to become the subject matter for a different class and species of pure Vaiṣṇavas, and therefore seem at great odds with our neophyte conditions. All of a sudden, we find ourselves being reduced to our factual position—that of being the most foolish, lowest, and insignificant creatures in the presence of your most exalted personality. This is again a manifestation of your divine mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that on this auspicious day, you are bestowing upon us the most important lesson of our entire education and giving us the most significant and important realization of the year.

We therefore call again upon you in our feeble way, to help us strengthen this realization day by day so that we may be forced to give up our outrageous views of our own mundane intelligence, wisdom, knowledge, and abilities. Only then will we be able to accept with full faith and confidence your divine intelligence, divine wisdom, divine knowledge, and divine instructions as the eternal guiding principles in our childish, neophyte existences. And only then will we be able to attain the most cherished goal of life, *kṛṣṇa-prema,* love of Godhead.

*Begging to attain the position of your eternal, insignificant disciples and granddisciples,*

## Eastern Europe

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracārine*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Please accept our humble obeisances on this -most auspicious day celebrating your divine appearance within this material world.

This material world is created by the dreamirig of Mahā-Viṣṇu, by His glance, and is being maintained by His energies and expansions.

This ISKCON world is created by your dreaming, Śrīla Prabhupāda; you have glanced upon all of us, and you are maintaining everything in perfect order through your expansions as books, instructions, *ācāryas* and GBC. Inconceivable!

Who but Your Divine Grace could predict that in all the Daityastan everyone would assemble and chant the holy name of Kṛṣṇa?

Who could present in such a perfect and absolute way *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is,* the all-embracing philosophy wherein anyone—whether he considers himself a socialist, communist or capitalist—will find the true basis of ideology and will be able to live in peace, harmony and transcendental bliss? Only you, most beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda!

So please cast your glance upon us continuously, so that we may continue executing the accomplishment of your divine mission, of your great and wonderful dream!

All glories to Your Divine Grace!

Śrīla Prabhupāda ki jaya!

*Your eternal servants in Graz and Eastern Europe,*

## East Lansing

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most indebted obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. And why are we indebted?

Because you personally went through so much hardship to come to our Western shores at such an elderly age to save us from the vicious cycle of *saṁsāra*.  
Because you personally taught us how to properly worship the Supreme Lord, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, by your own impeccable example.  
Because you so mercifully linked us with the eternal *guru-paramparā,* which is full of life and without which one only drowns in useless speculation.  
Because you gave us the holy name of Kṛṣṇa and taught us how to worship it as nondifferent from Kṛṣṇa Himself.  
Because you taught us how to cook and relish *prasādam*.  
Because you wrote so many books on the nature of the Absolute Truth for us to study and distribute, and through which we can remember Kṛṣṇa.  
Because you taught us how to dance for Kṛṣṇa, freeing us from the existence of temporary consciousness.  
Because you taught us how to be clean, physically and spiritually.  
Because you taught us the beauty of simple living and high thinking.  
Because you smiled at us and won our hearts.  
Because unlike everyone else, you repeatedly preached to us about the miseries of material life and the danger of sense gratification.  
Because you taught us how to be gentle, sober and giving.  
Because you gave us the personal form of Lord Kṛṣṇa and taught us how to worship, bathe and dress Him.  
Because by your very life, you taught us the inner nature of the meaning of selflessness.  
Because you created this wonderful ISKCON society so that we can associate with sincere souls who are interested in the genuine goal of life.  
Because so many of us were not qualified, and still you gave us *dīkṣā* and then personally suffered for our misdeeds.  
Because you gave us first-class God conscious schools for our children.  
Because you taught us how to have first-class children.  
Because you taught us about the wonderful nature of our mother, the cow.  
Because for our sake you exposed the cheaters of society (politicians, mundane philosophers, scientists, sectarian religionists), and to convince us you gave us a direct taste of the spiritual realm by this process of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.  
Because you were so benevolent that you would take whatever propensity a person had and engage it in devotional service.  
Because you loved us, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Dear divine master, this is only a minuscule amount of the great attributes that you embody. Please, during these troubled times on this earth planet, empower us so that we may spread this soul-saving knowledge for all of the world to embrace. Without you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are more helpless than straw in the midst of a tornado. Please allow us to always remember your lotus feet.

*Your insignificant servants,*

## Fortaleza

We offer our most humble obeisances unto our spiritual master, who enlightened us with the torchlight of knowledge when we were in the most obscure ignorance.

Remembering Śrīla Prabhupāda is very easy if you think and serve the movement and the devotees he has created. I have never had the chance to meet Śrīla Prabhupāda and serve him in a more personal way, although he once appeared to me in a dream and he smashed me because I was not chanting my rounds properly. So we can understand that the spiritual master is always present to protect his disciples. His mercy is the greatest of his unlimited qualities. When we get the chance to taste a little of this quality, then we can understand how important his mission is. Prahlāda Mahārāja said that he would only go back to Godhead if he could take all the rascals with him. Therefore we can understand that the spiritual master is eternal, and he is always ready to offer his mercy to many rascals like myself. Please never stop preaching to me.

*Your servants from Fortaleza Mandira,*

Durāśāda dāsa adhikārī / Puruṣa-purāṇa dāsa adhikārī / Nitya-sukhī dāsa brahmacārī / Bhakta William / Bhakta Carlos / Maitrī-devī dāsī / Tattvavit-devī dāsī / Kṛṣṇa-Murāri / Vaikuṇṭha-prasāda

## France

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

Yet another year has passed, and we again find ourselves pausing to reflect on your causeless mercy upon us. In the beginning you pioneered everything alone and patiently trained us for the day we would carry on without you. The day inevitably arrived, a new chapter began, years have since gone by, and as you desired, everything is expanding. Thus as one year passes we look forward to the next. Each year we see the mundane journalists publish the events of the previous year, complete with photographs to testify to the pain. But we find the devotees’ reports increase in their ecstasy as time goes on—and we see their smiling faces to prove it. So rather than destroy, time increases our good fortune by giving us the opportunity to serve you. In this way it has lost its powerful influence upon us as we eternally take shelter in you. Everything is changing under its control; and yet in Kṛṣṇa consciousness the original bliss you gave us remains constant, while ever-increasing! Please grant us the mercy that we might always be engaged in your service, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and never let us take for granted the precious gift of devotional service you have given us. Let us always remember you and thereby overcome all obstacles we might encounter. In the beginning, when we were completely in the clutches of māyā, just one moment’s association with you freed us from her tight hold—and it is through constant remembrance of you and your instructions that we will continue to be free of her grasp.

This year we achieved a big victory in your plan for Europe, with the purchase of the Chateau d’ Ermenonville. You once told us that if we were able to get five places in Europe like our New Māyāpur farm here in France, then we could easily spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness on this continent. So by acquiring the castle, that order is accomplished, and now we’re prepared to work on your master plan. Keeping at heart your original instruction to increase book distribution yearly, we find everything expanding beyond anyone’s imagination. Of course, for you, all these developments have always been a reality, not imagination. Thus the success of your International Society for Krishna Consciousness, despite all opposing elements, stands as proof of your position as *jagad-guru* and the Supreme Personality of Servitor Godhead. Though the world may be facing the most difficult times ahead, it is sure and certain that your movement will only continue to progress and provide shelter for everyone concerned. Therefore, as your devotees we are only optimistic about the great challenge of service which you have given us for the future. We realize the great responsibility that lies ahead for all of us, and we are depending fully on your mercy to give us the strength and determination to do as you desire—to give Kṛṣṇa consciousness to all the fallen conditioned souls. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

*Your eternal servants,*

**France Yātrā**

## Fullerton

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Now it’s our turn to try and glorify you,  
Our lord, our savior and eternal guru.  
You’re the ācārya for the entire wild human herd,  
And you tamed us with gulabs and sābji with curd.

After hearing attentively from your beloved guru,  
The only question you asked was “How shall I serve you?”  
He gave you the order to go preach in the West,  
And by carrying it out you far surpassed all the rest.

When you first arrived here, no one knew of you.  
Soon the whole world will realize you’re the jagad-guru.  
You preached like no one else has ever done,  
Shedding light everywhere, like the rays of the sun.

Some say, “One can learn about Kṛṣṇa from any guru,  
But we’re feeling satisfied just hearing from you.  
After all, to become Kṛṣṇa conscious and spiritually grow,  
You’ve already given us everything we need to know.

We pray to eternally be allowed to serve you,  
Assisting you in your service to your dear Śrī Guru.  
So begging for your mercy we thank you, Prabhupāda,  
For your books, the holy name, and of course, kṛṣṇa-prasāda.

*Your servants at New Santipura Dhāma,*

Haripāda dāsa adhikārī / Lohitākṣa dāsa brahmacārī / Bhakta Dave / Phalinī-devī dāsī / Chiman Kotecha and family / Pranjivan Popat and family / Bhakta Dennis / Bhakta Jim / Sasha / Bhaktin Grace

## Gauhati

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracārine*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

In *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* we have all read

What Mahāprabhu asked and Rāmānanda Rāya said,  
What was the most painful situation on earth.  
Nothing but separation from Kṛṣṇa’s pure devotee  
Was the only one to break one’s heart.  
So, Śrīla Prabhupāda, allow us your association  
Through all your pure instructions.  
You’ve showed by the example of your own life  
How every moment each one should strive  
To dedicate their own lives for preaching  
The message of Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s teaching.  
With whomever it was that you would meet,  
You managed it so well with words so sweet  
To change the whole conversation  
Nothing but to the Lord’s glorification.  
None of your disciples have you ever turned away,  
No matter how offensive was their way.  
Unparalleled is your kindness, compassion, and expertise  
To engage the mostfallen ones in Kṛṣṇa’s service.  
Your humility has shattered my fallen pride.  
All your lectures, so daring and bold,  
Have enthused your disciples to go far and wide,  
To lay their lives and preach as you told.

On this auspicious day, O my lord and master devine, I fall at your feet and thank you for allowing me to try to assist you!

Your servants,

Bhavabhūti dāsa / Vaikuṇṭha dāsa / Sāmba dāsa / Revatīnandana dāsa / Bāla-Kṛṣṇa dāsa / Kāmākhya dāsa / Narasiṁha-mūrti dāsa / Maṅgalā-gopī-devī dāsī / Subhadrā Yogamāyā-devī dāsī / Vṛṣabhānu-devī dāsī / Rukmiṇī-rāṇī-devī dāsī / and the teachers and students of The Bhaktivedanta Hilltop School

## Germany

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracārine*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On this day of your auspicious appearance before us in this world of ignorance, we humbly strive to glorify Your Divine Grace as the representative of Śrīla Vyāsadeva, the compiler of the Vedic scriptures and the literary incarnation of Godhead. But as we stand before you, numb in your awe-inspiring presence, feeling the heavy weight of your grave intenseness pressing down upon our too-frivolous hearts, we are simply bewildered at how we have been so fortunate as to attain your association.

What are we to make of this epithet, “representative of Vyāsa”—we who are lowborn and devoid of culture, we who are totally outside the purview of Vedic reference? For us, you are all we know about Śrīla Vyāsadeva and the scriptures he has compiled. Had you not left the holy land of Vṛndāvana for the purpose of preaching in the Western world, the words *Śrīla Vyāsadeva* or *Vedic scripture* or *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* would have absolutely no meaning for us. As far as we are concerned, it is you who have compiled the Vedic scriptures in a way that is understandable to the common men in this age of Kali. It is you who have pointed out the supreme good—devotional service to Śrī Kṛṣṇa—from amongst the seemingly confused tangle of doctrines espoused by various sages down through the ages. It is you who have established the nectarean pastimes of Śrī Hari as being the topmost literary subject matter, the essence of all poetic descriptions, the most potent object of meditation.

In your books we find that the only practical means for developing love of Kṛṣṇa in this age is the process of *hari-nāma saṅkīrtana* (congregational chanting of the holy name of Kṛṣṇa). We find again and again that Śrīla Vyāsadeva has made reference to chanting the holy name in the Kali-yuga throughout the entire body of Puranic literature. We find furthermore that the *avatāra* of the age of Kali can be understood by his symptoms—that is, as being the foremost preacher of the holy name of the Lord. And although we acknowledge and respect your modest presentation of yourself as being the most insignificant servant of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, we must nonetheless declare that for our unworthy, fallen selves, it is you who are the embodiment of the *saṅkīrtana po*tency that defines the *avatāra* of the holy name. It is from your books that we read about the *saṅkīrtana yuga-dharma,* and it is from your shining example as the founder-*ācārya* of the International Society for Krishna Consciousness that we know how the *saṅkīrtana* movement is to be practically spread around the world to fulfill the prophecy of Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

We wish that we could see deeper into the mystery of your identity, Śrīla Prabhupāda. We wish that we had served you better so that we might be blessed with a clearer understanding of your glories, simply so that on this holy day we could better describe them. But our vision is too narrow, our deficiencies too great. All we can say is: Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are everything for us. Had you not appeared, there would be no Kṛṣṇa consciousness available to us anywhere in the world.

*Your servants in ISKCON Germany,*  
The devotees of Heidelberg, Munich, Freiberg and the Nava-Jiyaḍa-Nṛsiṁha-Kṣetra Farm

## Gītā-nāgarī

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. I want to thank you for giving us your mercy. As time goes on, we are realizing more and more that your mercy is unlimited and that our appreciation of it will grow as long as we continue to follow your divine instructions. Actually, your mercy is that you are engaging us in Kṛṣṇa’s service. Because you are such a pure devotee and, as you always said, you are purely following your spiritual master, so we become pure by following your instructions, and we can transmit the purity to others by our example. You have created a tree that is growing many branches simply by your potency, and I can see that if we continue to accept your mercy by performing devotional service under your guidance, then this tree will continue to grow unlimitedly and the prophecy of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu will be fulfilled, by your mercy.

I know you have no desire whatsoever to be glorified. You only want to see Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead, glorified. That is your whole mission. So when we glorify Kṛṣṇa and teach other people to glorify Kṛṣṇa, then you become happy beyond our material calculation. From our vantage point we cannot understand your relationship with Kṛṣṇa. But we know one thing—that out of your causeless mercy you picked us up out of the ocean of material existence. And if we hang on to the lifeline of devotional service that you have given us, then we are safe. You have introduced us to your dear friend, Lord Kṛṣṇa. I hope that we don’t embarrass you in any way by offending Him. You risked everything for the glory of your Lord, Kṛṣṇa, and emerged victorious, the hero of the century. And you left a legacy of heroes, the GBC, who are valiantly bearing your flag on the battlefield, risking everything, as you showed them by your example.

So I would like to take the opportunity on this auspicious occasion of your appearance in this world to place our arrogant free will at your lotus feet, praying that it will be purified in the fire of devotional service to Lord Kṛṣṇa, which is the manifestation of your mercy on us.

*Your servants*,

The devotees of Gītā-nāgarī,

## Glasgow

To His Divine Grace Oṁ Viṣṇupāda Paramahaṁsa Parivrājakācārya 108 Śrī Śrīmad Abhaya Caraṇāravinda Bhaktivedanta Swami Mahārāja Prabhupāda. Our beloved spiritual master and savior.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracārine*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

On this most auspicious occasion, *we the undersigned pledge our eternal allegiance to the service of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead.* He is the highest truth, the original person, the ultimate destination, the dearmost friend, and at the same time, He is everything. These facts are confirmed in the *Vedānta-sūtra, Bhāgavata Purāṇa, Bhagavad-gītā,* the *Upaniṣads* and other authentic scriptures.

However, there is only one way to attain self-realization, or Kṛṣṇa consciousness, in this age, and that is by following the simple formula given and exhibited by Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu: chant the holy names of the Lord, chant the holy names of the Lord, chant the holy names of the Lord! So *we also pledge our eternal allegiance to Lord Caitanya,* the golden *avatāra,* the infallible Supreme Godhead who appeared as a devotee to teach us how to love Kṛṣṇa.

However, the fallen souls of five thousand years after Kali-yuga’s start could not recognize or appreciate Lord Caitanya or His sublime mission. It was only your divine intervention that saved us. Therefore, *we pledge our eternal allegiance to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda,* who have demonstrated infallible devotional service to Lord Caitanya and Lord Kṛṣṇa.

Following you means following your instructions and your exemplary behavior; however, without the constant association of devotees, we will misunderstand you. You created the International Society for Krishna Consciousness to perpetuate the mission of Lord Caitanya. Therefore *we pledge our allegiance to ISKCON,* the sacred institution meant to elevate everyone of this world back home, back to Godhead. Some say ISKCON is only a name. But we say that as long as ISKCON acts on the principles of “Books are the basis \[your books, Śrīla Prabhupāda\]; purity is the force; preaching is the essence; and utility is the principle,” ISKCON is as good as Kṛṣṇa Himself. It is our firm resolve today, nearly five years after your disappearance, that indeed ISKCON is proceeding steadily ahead in all four of these areas, undaunted by challenges. To insure that ISKCON continues to act on these principles, the Governing Body Commission (GBC) was formed by you. Therefore, *we pledge allegiance to this group of truly holy souls dedicated to propagate and protect your movement.* We pray that they may continue to be showered with your unlimited, causeless mercy.

All glories to Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa! All glories to Lord Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu! All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda! All glories to ISKCON! All glories to ISKCON GBC!

*Your humble servants,*  
Danavīra dāsa / Balabhadra dāsa / Vāmanadeva dāsa / Bhakta Alistair / Bhakta Tom / Dvārakā Dave

FOLK

David Porter / Vince Morton / Jim Bucchanan, Nadine, Maria / Jenny Smith / Billy Laughlan and family / Stuart Coyle and family / Richard Fairley / Russell Lockhart / The Piericcinis

## Guadalajara

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracārine*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please permit us to offer our insignificant obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the bona fide representative of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa; therefore you are disclosing the conclusion of the revealed scriptures—devotional service to the Supreme Lord, Śrī Kṛṣṇa. In this way you are helping the conditioned souls to reestablish their lost but eternal relationship with Kṛṣṇa.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, *patita-pāvana,* you are the friend and benefactor of the most fallen, the manifest representative of Lord Nityānanda Prabhu, and as such you are empowered to deliver unlimited numbers of conditioned souls. Lord Nityānanda delivered Jagāi and Mādhāi, but by His Grace His pure servant can liberate tens of millions of Jagāis and Mādhāis. By your loving guidance you are freeing us from our sinful condition, and for this we wish to eternally offer you our insignificant obeisances. Jaya, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Please give us your blessing so that we may always remember our eternal debt to you.

By your causeless mercy we are able to live in your temples, associate with your devotees, see daily the *arcā-vigraha* form of the Lord, participate in ecstatic *kīrtanas,* read the revealed scriptures such as *Bhagavad-gītā* and *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* put on wonderful festivals such as Rathayātrā, partake of *Kṛṣṇa-prasādam,* and faithfully engage in executing your desire to propagate the *saṅkīrtana* movement and serve your ISKCON mission.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

*Your unworthy servants at the Guadalajara Mandira.*
