## Gujarat

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

It is now the fifth year of your disappearance, and we still feel as if you were here just yesterday. We are surrounded by your memories, your books, your lecture tapes, your *kīrtanas,* your disciples—and with the pangs of your separation as intense as they were four and a half years ago. It was your specific desire to train us to always take more shelter of your *vāṇī* (instruction form) than your *vapuh* (physical form). Of course, there is no difference between the two, as they are both on the absolute platform. But since *vāṇī* eternally lives with the disciple, you always stressed more your instructions. After so many years, we are beginning to realize that you are not gone, that you are always present, that you never felt separated from your spiritual master. We can also eternally be with you if we simply go on cherishing the things that were dearmost to you, the people you most loved, the tunes you sang most, the *ślokas* you quoted most, the *prasādam* you liked most. Indeed, we feel most fortunate that by your grace we have developed some appreciation for these original Prabhupāda tastes. Therefore, any other tastes, speeches, discourses, dialogues, any other personalities that are not strictly in Prabhupāda taste—they do not hold any attraction for us. Even though we didn’t have any austerity, penance, charity, cleanliness, sense control, tolerance, or mercy to our credit, and even though our hearts were parched with material desires, somehow or other we could wonder, “Who has brought this revolution in the demoniac consciousness of the world? Who is this person? Who is this person with unbounded compassion for all the fallen souls?” Just by this little awe and reverence for Your Divine Grace, an impossible miracle happened. Our deeply materialistic consciousness was disturbed. Our material ambitions were frustrated. Our material hopes were baffled, and we were forced to come under the divine shelter of your lotus feet.

Prahlāda Mahārāja describes this as the *only* way for a materialistic man to come to the lotus feet of Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

*naiṣāṁ matis tāvad urukramāṅghriṁ*  
*spṛśaty anarthāpagamo yad-arthaḥ*  
*mahīyasāṁ pāda-rajo-'bhiṣekaṁ*  
*niṣkiñcanānāṁ na vṛṇīta yāvat*

**Unless they smear upon their bodies the dust of the lotus feet of a Vaiṣṇava completely freed from material contamination, persons very much inclined toward materialistic life cannot be attached to the lotus feet of the Lord, who is glorified for His uncommon activities. Only by becoming Kṛṣṇa conscious and taking shelter at the lotus feet of the Lord in this way can one be freed from material contamination.** ***(Bhāg.*** **7.5.32)**

Envious people do not understand the ways and means of Kṛṣṇa’s representative, and they try to find faults in an indirect way, since they cannot openly find fault. They make some word jugglery and try to prove their superiority. Only very foolish people fall for these counterfeit representatives and thereby lose your grace and the grace of the disciplic succession. Therefore, it is very important not to make any experiments with the desire of the *guru. As* Narottama dāsa Ṭhākura says, *gurumukha-padma-vākya, cittete koriyā aikya, ār nā koriho mane āśā:* “The disciple should not even think, in his mind, of anything other than the order of the *guru.”*

You always told us that if you had any credit, it was that you never deviated from the instruction of your *guru.* Śrīla Bhaktisiddhanta said, “If you get money, then just print books and distribute them.” When you were in India you had very little money, and you were printing *Back to Godhead* and distributing it to as many people as possible. Even after starting a big, worldwide mission, your main emphasis was to publish books in as many languages as possible and distribute them all over the world. This is firm faith in the words of the spiritual master. You always told us that we were very naive and that we could be easily misled. Just as there was Pauṇḍraka who imitated Kṛṣṇa by artificially holding the four symbols of Viṣṇu, similarly we have to beware of so many pseudo spiritual masters, who haven’t even made one devotee. They cannot even change the life of one person, and here they dare to compare themselves with *you.* This is very tragic. We have a long battle to fight with all-powerful *māyā.* Our only hope is you, your instructions and your true representatives. If one of your generals loses faith in your weapons and turns to some other commander, thinking you are gone, it will be a great setback for our forces against *māyā.* For you said, if one commander says only one thing different, then there would be chaos. This is the time of testing the loyalty to your lotus feet. You have left everything necessary for one to become Kṛṣṇa conscious—your books, your instructions, your preaching methods. Kṛṣṇa consciousness is perfect and complete, and you are also perfect and complete. For a sincere disciple there is no need to go anywhere. Your lotus feet are our only shelter. Once you told us that our love for you would be manifested by the cooperative spirit with which we would work together to push on your mission. This ISKCON is your mission. It is nondifferent from you. Due to mundane vision, one may see discrepancies in it. But such people saw these same “defects” when you were present also. This Kali-yuga is an ocean of faults for one who wants to see faults. You nurtured this ISKCON with great care. You built it with great tolerance and determination. Now we are realizing that our foremost commitment to you is to know that this structure you have built is nondifferent from you. To make a break in that structure is to break your very body. You warned us yourself that no outside force can check your ISKCON. Only disharmony from within can destroy your ISKCON. Please give us the strength to not offend or humiliate you, to become the servant of your servant, to always remember your desires and never forget them, and to at least maintain what you have built, if not expand it. Even though we are devoid of spiritual strength, just by its transcendental nature your movement is spreading all over Gujarat. We have seen that by our remaining steady and fixed in your service, Kṛṣṇa reciprocates hundreds of times over, and transcendental results come about. For example, it was once our sentimental desire to have your picture in everyone’s household in India, and by Kṛṣṇa’s mercy one member just ordered a hundred thousand calendars with your picture on the front page. This will be displayed in different households throughout the country. We are completely foolish and ignorant. We have no knowledge of Kṛṣṇa, nor do we have any desire to know Kṛṣṇa. We simply want to remain at your lotus feet life after life and aspire to serve you in your mission, in whatever way it may be.

*Begging to remain your servants,*  
Your disciples and granddisciples of ISKCON Gujarat

## Guyana

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On behalf of the devotees at New Pānihāṭi and ISKCON Linepath “E,” please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. On this most auspicious day we meditate on your transcendentally glorious qualities and pastimes in this world. Your *lī1ā* is effulgent like the sun and is dissipating the darkness of ignorance in this deep, dark world. By meditating on you, even the most hardhearted, lowborn rascals like ourselves are coming to appreciate what you have done and are continuing to do. We understand that any success we may have is due to what you have left us.

We cannot glorify you properly because of our constant offenses and lack of spiritual vision. Great personalities glorify you as *jagad-guru* throughout the universe. Here we are endeavoring to preach the message of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. By following your instructions strictly we know that you will be pleased and will benedict us with a drop of your ocean of mercy.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please give us the strength to remember you at every moment. This will be the perfection of our lives.

Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda.

*Your servants at New Pānihāṭi and ISKCON Linepath,*

New Pānihāṭi

## Haridaspur

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracārine*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Just as Haridāsa Ṭhākura engaged everyone in chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa, Your Divine Grace engaged the whole world in chanting the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra* and dancing. Therefore we fall at your lotus feet in obeisances, offering our service and surrender.

As Your Divine Grace has desired a temple to be constructed for the glory of Haridāsa Ṭhākura at this sacred *śrīpat,* gradually the pure devotee’s desires are manifesting. We always look forward to your return in Haridaspur, which you visited at least twice. Since that may not be our good fortune, now we would like to observe the day of your visiting Haridaspur some time in the future—since even the momentary presence of a pure devotee at once purifies that place and makes it a *tīrtha* for all time. Thus, now Haridaspur is doubly sanctified, as both the Nāmācārya and Your Divine Grace have graced this humble village of Gaura-maṇḍala-bhūmi.

All glories to the *paramahaṁsa parivrājakācārya* empowered devotee Śrīla Prabhupāda—our shelter and inspiration!

*Clinging to your lotus feet,*

Jayapatāka Swami / Bhakti-prabhava Swami / Tīrthapāda dāsa brahmacārī / Gaura-Govinda dāsa brahmacārī / Śyāma-Kṛṣṇa dāsa brahmacārī / Acyuta-Kṛṣṇa dāsa adhikārī / Arjuna dāsa / Vraja-priya-devī dāsī / Vraja-latā-devī dāsī / Hlādinī Śakti-devī dāsī / Nitya-sevā-devī dāsī / Govinda, Gopala, Suresh and all the Haridaspur *grāmavāsī* villagers

## Harmony Farm

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracārine*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Falling at vour lotus feet we offer our obeisances to you.

Because of your intense love for your spiritual master, His Divine Grace Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Prabhupāda, and your bountiful compassion for all fallen sons of our eternal father, we have been causelessly benedicted with the most rare opportunity afforded to any living entity anywhere within all the worlds—that is, love for Kṛṣṇa, God, and devotional service in loving reciprocation with the supreme personality we’ve been looking for and hankering to serve for the past multitudes of lifetimes since time immemorial.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you know the heartfelt desires of your spiritual master to deliver every lost soul without discrimination; therefore you have overcome all material boundaries and surpassed all previous endeavors for spreading the cult of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. You have extended the grace of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu without limit to the three worlds, and it will continue to increase until all are enveloped by this ocean of your mercy.

This ocean is filled with your transcendental literatures, which are becoming more and more available in practically every language of this world. So potent and powerful are these books that simply by reading one word or touching one of these books, or even just seeing one of them, a man’s material life is as good as finished. The immensity of this gift of yours, Śrīla Prabhupāda, exceeds all material calculations.

Then you’ve extended the gift of sacred *prasādam* from the land of devotion to the very places and people that were once verily eating only sin. The vast *prasādam* distribution within the ranks of Śrī ISKCON, set up by Your Divine Grace, is in itself worthy of all our indebtedness to you. This boundless mercy of your giving to the world is unparalleled in transcendental potency. So marvelous it is that even a morsel of *kṛṣṇa-prasādam,* when tasted, is capable of making a dog stand up and dance in ecstasy.

Then, because of your deep love and affection for your spiritual master and your desire to have his glories sung and heard throughout the planet, you personally established the magnanimous International Society for Krishna Consciousness, with its multifarious fruits in the forms of temples, restaurants, traveling buses and boats, and farm communities. Because of this intense love you have for Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta, and your pure desire to serve and please him, the most glorious creation within the life-spans of decades and centuries of living entities was formed in the shape of ISKCON, and it is growing and growing, and it will continue to grow until every single person on every continent, in every town and village throughout the entire world, nay universe, will hear the tumultuous echo of the transcendental vibration of the holy names—Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare / Hare Rāma, Hare, Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare. And all entities-the birds, the beasts, the aquatics and the microscopic entities, as well as all humanity—will willingly or unwillingly be given the sweetest song, the rarest, most prolific gift, the highest and most profound activity possible, in this gift of *hari-nāma-saṅkīrtana.* And that is the greatest contribution to the world. It is the means and the end. All things are contained in the holy names. All opulences are manifest. All desires are fulfilled, and all entities can once again live together for the one and only purpose of our existence and the perfection of harmony.

Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, let us, all your children and grandchildren together, always remember you and never forget you, and let us always remain humble, menial servants at your soft lotus feet.

Hāsyagrāmī dāsa brahmacārī / Mathurā-pati dāsa brahmacārī / Raṇacora dāsa brahmacārī / Gopāla dāsa brahmacārī / Vāmanadeva dāsa adhikārī, Indīra-devī dāsī, Jagannātha, Gopīnātha, Vṛndā

## Hartford

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. All glories to your lotus feet.

*daivī hy esā guṇamayī mama māyā duratyayā*  
*mām eva ye prapadyante māyām etāṁ taranti te*

**“This divine energy of Mine, consisting of the three modes of material nature, is difficult to overcome. But those who have surrendered to Me can easily cross beyond it.” (Bg. 7.14)**

If we want freedom from the control of material nature, we must surrender to Kṛṣṇa, and the only way to surrender unto Kṛṣṇa is to surrender unto you.

*yasya prasādād bhagavat-prasādo*  
*yasyāprasādān na gatiḥ kuto 'pi*

To fully surrender unto you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, is to push forward your ISKCON movement twenty-four hours a day. To allow even a moment to pass without using it for you is *māyā.*

You once said, “Idle time is the devil’s workshop.” But by your mercy we have no time to be idle; we have unlimited engagement, and so we pray for the enthusiasm to take advantage of this wonderful service you have given us.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the most merciful, loving father anyone could ever have. We are so fortunate to have you as our *guru.* Others may choose to serve *māyā,* and for their faithful service they simply get suffering, birth after birth. But we choose to serve you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and we attain the spiritual world—eternity, knowledge and bliss. Even more than this, we attain the pleasure of pleasing you, the satisfaction of satisfying you and the happiness of seeing you happy. This is the taste Kṛṣṇa speaks of in the *Bhagavad-gītā: paraṁ dṛṣṭvā nivartate.*

We pray unto your lotus feet, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that we may always be engaged in serving you, always be engaged in pleasing you and always be engaged in thoughts of you.

We take shelter at your lotus feet.

Śrīla Prabhupāda ki jaya.

*Your eternal servants at Hartford ISKCON,*

Pyāri Mohana dāsa / Raktaka dāsa / Ānanda dāsa / Padmanābha dāsa / Jīvanauṣadhi-devī dāsī / Kālindī-devī dāsī / Bhakta Russ / Polly Perlmutter

## Hawaii

Our many lives have come and gone; they all were full of pain.  
A struggle we forever gave, but defeat always came.  
A desire to live eternally with friends and family,  
but time would always end it all with recurring tragedy.  
Our leaders could not help a bit; solutions they’d not find.  
Still, foolishly they point the way—“the blind leading the blind.”  
So what were we to do in life except to waste away,  
not knowing of the goal of life or to whom we should pray?  
But then by your compassion and sweet qualities divine,  
you came to give real knowledge, like the dazzling bright sunshine.  
You always had a loving touch which always made our day,  
you made a plan to save us all in Lord Caitanya’s way.  
So many books you’ve given us to guide us once you’ve gone,  
but in those books you still reside to keep us always strong.  
And in your ISKCON temples still you live eternally to distribute the sweet fruits from the Lord Caitanya tree.  
So now our lives we give to you for you to take control.  
You are our only shelter and our very life and soul.

*Your loving servants in the Hawaiian Islands,*

## Hong Kong

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet, which are traversing this entire cosmic manifestation, giving shelter to so many fallen conditioned souls.

You always said, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that there were two ways of associating with the spiritual master: through his physical presence *(vapuḥ)* and through his instructions *(vāṇī)*. You taught us that the most important of the two was *vāṇī* association, because we can always associate with the spiritual master through his instructions, whereas he may not always be physically present. Now we are fully realizing this fact that, although your physical form has been absent from our vision for almost five years, we still feel you personally guiding ISKCON through your instructions.

Actually, Śrīla Prabhupāda, any activity which we are performing within this movement is simply due to your causeless mercy. You have taught us everything: how to chant, how to worship the Deity, how to dance, how to prepare and honor *prasādam,* how to preach, and ultimately, how to mold our lives in such a way that every activity we perform is reviving our loving relationship with the Supreme Personality of Godhead.

You have given us your life as a perfect example, so that we may always be absorbed in your transcendental pastimes. It is these nectarean pastimes that are our constant source of renewed enthusiasm and spiritual strength. When we think of how you were an embodiment of a whole Kṛṣṇa conscious culture, and how you singlehandedly implanted that culture in the West, we are simply amazed. No ordinary living entity could have possibly done this—only an empowered representative of Kṛṣṇa, a *śaktyāveśa-avatāra.*

Through the menial and insignificant service that we are trying to render, we will never be able to repay you for what you have given us. Our only prayer is that we can somehow or other try to fulfill your innermost desires—to publish and distribute your books profusely and to make devotees, giving everyone in Hong Kong and China your gift of Krpa consciousness.

*We beg to remain your eternal servants,*

Giridhārī dāsa brahmacārī / Rāmaśaraṇa dāsa brahmacārī / Hanumān dāsa brahmacārī / Ekādaśī dāsa brahmacārī / Devakīnandana dāsa brahmacārī / Jitarati dāsa adhikārī / Yaśomatī-suta dāsa adhikārī / Kṛṣṇa Caitanya dāsa adhikārī / Gaura-vāṇī dāsa adhikārī / Tulasī dāsa adhikārī / Bhakta Rajesh /Māninī-devī dāsī / Sādhana-devī dāsī / Bṛhad-mṛdaṅga-devī dāsī

## Houston

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most heartfelt obeisances. All of us in Houston are spiritually young, and most of us are very young. Still, we are begging you to use us to fulfill your plans to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world. We are begging you to make our hearts clean and help us kick out all our desires for independence and sense gratification, that we may become qualified to preach the message of Lord Kṛṣṇa and sing your glories to the fallen, conditioned souls.

Your mercy is manifested through this ISKCON branch of the Caitanya tree. Through your books and your senior disciples, Lord Caitanya’s *saṅkīrtana* movement is ever increasing. You have set up the framework for this branch to provide for the spiritual welfare not only of ourselves, but for future generations for thousands of years to come.

You have said that your only credit is that you have carried out the order of your spiritual master. In the same way that Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu never claimed to be anything but the servant of the servant of the servant of Kṛṣṇa, you have shown by example that to carry out exactly the order of the spiritual master guarantees success. Further examples of this principle of clinging to the spiritual master’s orders as one’s life and soul are being shown by your senior disciples and the success they have had in pushing forward Kṛṣṇa consciousness in their respective zones.

All of our lives have been changed by you. We were totally engrossed in chewing the chewed and were miserably carrying on our existence, chasing after the so-called happiness of material life. But by Kṛṣṇa’s mercy we have met you, and now the purpose of this human form of life is clear.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we owe everything to you for your books, your creation of ISKCON and for your causeless mercy in allowing us to assist you in spreading this *saṅkīrtana* movement to every town and village. Please allow us to remain,

*The humble servants of your servants,*

## Hyannis

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

It was you who taught me simplified living so that I would have room to devote time for higher thinking leading me to the knowledge and pure experiences of loving God, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, through eagerly performing loving devotional service to Him and His associates.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you taught me how to perform my occupational duty with Śrī Kṛṣṇa in mind, and now although to the uninformed it still looks like ordinary *karmī* work, Śrī Kṛṣṇa and you know how to utilize me through my work and everyday living in such a way that anyone in this area whom you want to hear about your transcendental qualities and pastimes may have access to them here along with learning the gradual process of purification leading to self-realization.

Śrīla Prabhupāda

I want to thank you*,* Śrīla Prabhupāda, for translating the wisdom of the *Vedas* in such a way that even an academically uneducated person like myself can grasp the importance of applying the knowledge in your books to untangle me from the noose of materialism, and somehow or other force me above even the influence of the materialistic modes of goodness and passion and ignorance more and more. Thank you very much.

*Your indebted disciples at ISKCON Hyannis*

## Hyderabad

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
out of Kṛṣṇa’s own will so sweet  
at a time so proper and meet  
to deliver caṇḍālas, mlecchas, an unimaginable feat,  
you appeared and became the cause of *māyā’s* retreat.  
With flags of victory (Jayapatāka) behind you we march,  
Brahmānanda, Harikeśa, Svarūpa-Dāmodara, Manipura’s saintly monarch,  
Many other stalwarts empowered by Your Divine Grace  
to save human society from utter disgrace.  
Scientists, atheists, with great speed do flee,  
hearing your invincible arguments against their plea  
of creating life in the future, to date they have not created a flea,  
still with Darwin they will foolishly agree.  
Untold would the message of Caitanya have remained  
while resources of the earth drained.  
How could the insane inhabitants of earth  
have known that for the soul there is no death or birth?

Feelings of separation, a few fond memories of personal association sustain our very existence, your glorious mission.

In the hopes of vain fame and glory  
sad and painful to narrate is our story  
until we saw your feet divine.  
Let the right to serve you be mine.  
Oh master, you are our very life and soul,  
kindly allow us to serve you eternally, we have no other goal.  
Oh, for the day when all concoctions having been abandoned,  
the conditioned souls at your lotus feet be surrendered,  
giving up enviousness and false pride,  
the boat of your lotus feet ride.

Your eternal servants,

Mahāmān dāsa adhikārī / Pratāparudra dāsa adhikārī / Nīlāmbara dāsa adhikārī / Vāmana dāsa adhikārī / Amiya-vīlāsa dāsa brahmacārī / Nanda-kumāra dāsa brahmacārī / Lakṣmīnātha dāsa brahmacārī / Ācāryaratna dāsa brahmacārī / Godruma dāsa brahmacārī / Kṛṣṇa-kiśora dāsa brahmacārī / Rāmānanda Rāya dāsa brahmacārī / Vanamālī dāsa brahmacārī / Kāliya Kṛṣṇa dāsa brahmacārī / Rādhā-ramaṇa dāsa brahmacārī / Bhakta Vasudeva / Bhakta Guru Murti / Bhakta Veera / Bhakta Mohan / Paramahaṁsa-devī dāsī / Kṛṣṇa-kathā-devī dāsī / Bhakta Srinivas / Bhakta Anuraga

## Ireland

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances.

If we experience difficulties in our preaching work, then we hear you: “Do not be disturbed if people do not hear this message,” you said. “We will say it to the moon, we will say to the stars, we will say it to all directions. . . .” These words of yours are so enlivening to us.

How unbreakable is your determination to spread the message of Kṛṣṇa. Witnessing your life by reading your biography, we are simply amazed and want to “do as you were doing.”

Once we asked you if we could close a temple and rather open smaller centers in other cities, since the place appeared unmanageable and impractical. You wouldn’t give an inch and share our *māyā.* “If your child has been born deaf and blind, does it mean you kill it?” you thundered. “No.” And then you said after the silence, “Don’t kill it. Please develop it. Don’t become rolling stones. Set moss on.”

We were amazed at the strength of your determination. How fearless—the unshaken rock to build your ISKCON.

Let us always be at your service.

## Israel

By nature’s own way The soul faces a system  
Divinely contrived To perplex and resist him.  
As forest fire blazes Which none has effected,  
So problems come that he Neither wants nor expected.  
It therefore behooves him To seek the solution  
And not to remain in This state of confusion.  
In *Bhagavad-gītā* Lord Kṛṣṇa’s suggestion:  
Approach a pure soul To answer your question.  
As inquiry and service Establish reliance  
The spiritual master Reveals the great science.  
The guru wields truth As the swordsman his blade,  
To slash the tight knots that Illusion has made.  
Thus freed, the disciple Sees clearly his purpose  
To satisfy Kṛṣṇa By pure loving service.  
He worships his guru Along with his Lord and,  
As gratitude drives him, Takes up the same sword.  
When Prabhupāda came He delivered so many  
Whose qualification Was not worth a penny.  
He brought them together In beautiful temples;  
He taught them with books Full of dazzling examples.  
He trained his men well—How to act—what to say—  
To bring the world under Mahāprabhu’s sway.  
Then Prabhupāda’s preachers Went out thus inspired  
To distribute the mercy As he so desired.  
The demons would argue And snarl and shout,  
But Prabhupāda’s victory Was never in doubt.  
So lofty his vision, So noble his plan,  
To fan the small spark in The heart of each man.  
Such preaching, he showed, Is the price one must pay  
To enter Vṛndāvana At the end of the day.  
... And then he departed In exactly that way.

*Your servants in Israel,*

Ayodhyā-pati dāsa / Batu Gopāla dāsa / Guṇa-avatāra dāsa / Nitya-siddha dāsa / Nitya-līlā dāsa / Premāñjana dāsa / Bhakta Iti / Guru-Bhaktā-devī dāsī / Bhūmi-devī dāsī / Jalāṅgī-devī dāsī / Devahūti-devī dāsī / Rāma-līlā-devī dāsī / Rādhā Mādhava-devī dāsī / Bhaktāmṛta-devī dāsī / Mahā-lakṣmī-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Vered.

## Italy

Living in the grim compound of the city of the nine gates,  
no more way we could get out from the prisons of our fates.  
The stupidity of our lives made us think we were so wise,  
cursed by lust and greed to die with hallucinated minds.  
Almost ready to black out, when a swan flew near about,  
slightly touched us with his wings, brought us worlds of ecstasies.  
In the transcendental sphere Kṛṣṇa told you to come down,  
speak to us nectarean words, swan like man with gracious glance.  
Avatāra of gentleness, moved by our extreme disgrace,  
ghosts with lots of eagerness for a suicidal race.  
By the greatest miracle you have been visible to us;  
smile to us and joke with us, make us dance and take prasād.  
But now Kṛṣṇa has called you back to submit your full report.  
“Are these rascals coming back to the transcendental world?”  
Though we are without a hope, we just pray you, Prabhupāda,  
let us understand your books, strive for walking by your side.  
Great Arjuna could not bear strongest sense of emptiness  
when he found himself still here without Kṛṣṇa’s loveliness.  
Same we’re feeling in these days, small birds fallen from the nest.  
Māyā is trying to find her ways how to stop your ISKCON best. Cooperation is the way to fulfill all your desiies;  
Lord Caitanya’s splendent rays over all these fools and liars.  
Whether you give us great joy by being present before us  
or you make us brokenhearted disappearing ftom our sight,  
You remain the eternal master dearmost friend and loving guide, founder of Caitanya era, glorious Śrīla Prabhupāda.

ŚRĪLA PRABHUPĀDA KI JAYA

## Jakarta

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracārine*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

From beyond this dark world and its three modes which bind us,  
Where you served with His servitors the Lord of Vṛndāvana  
Out of love you descended, just to remind us  
Of our own love for Kṛṣṇa, which we had forgotten.

You heard with submission and served with full vigor  
Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta, the Lord’s empowered teacher.  
Many men sat in temples; your ideas were much bigger.  
Print books, then distribute; let each man become preacher.

You chanted the glories of Kṛṣṇa and Rādhā  
From the dhāma to the West, from the West back to Bhārata.  
You spread mahā-mantra far and wide, like Nārada,  
And your books are acclaimed by each scholar and paṇḍita.

You remembered the Lord, both in gain and in hardships,  
Accepting all things as His mercy and kindness,  
With your mind always fixed on the feet of Their Lordships  
And Their nectarean pastimes, which slake this world’s dryness.

You served the Lord’s feet, found the best for His pleasure.  
Not just flowers, but new bhaktas, you placed all before Him,  
And revealing to all about life’s greatest treasure,  
Satisfying the Lord beyond all understanding.

You worshiped the Lord in His form in the temple,  
Establishing mandiras in the lands of the fallen,  
Teaching how to be free from all problems mental.  
“Serve the Lord with the senses, become free from all tension."

You prayed to the Lord by pure words you chose carefully,  
Pleased the Lord by sincerity, changed the hearts of the hearers  
With no other desire than to serve Him eternally.  
Of all pure devotees, there is none who is dearer.

You executed Kṛṣṇa’s orders, obtaining His grace  
With such expertise that the world may be saved,  
Establishing ISKCON in every place.  
Of all the great heroes, you are the most brave.

As a friend of the Lord, you are the most dear.  
You taught everyone about their dear most friend,  
Taking much inconvenience to come to us here,  
So we too may remember our Lord at the end.

You surrendered all to Kṛṣṇa till you left for Goloka.  
In your service to Kṛṣṇa with mind, word and action,  
By the sādhus, the guru and śāstra, each śloka,  
You taught the world how to be free from reaction.

Although I’m not worthy to be your disciple,  
I hope this may please you on this holy day.  
Though I have no good qualities that to you may be likable.  
Please bless me that I still may serve you in some way.

Your servants of Jakarta, Indonesia *Yātr*ā,

## Kathmandu

**Science of the Soul**

The science of the soul, known to very few,  
So many lives wasted in not knowing what to do,  
Engaging in acts that only go against the soul  
Birth after birth, diseased and growing old.  
Spiritual by nature, but in the grip of māyās hand  
Illusioned into thinking this world’s at our command,  
On the mental platform of duality  
Never seeing that we’re all part of the Supreme.  
The science of the soul seems to be lost—  
Simply trying to enjoy this world at any cost—  
But is it really worth the price we have to pay  
When death comes along to take our soul away?  
“No God in control, “ I hear you falsely claim.  
But by what do you measure your happiness and your pain?  
You put it down to nature, and that’s all you have to say.  
With no thought of tomorrow, you live for today.  
The science of the soul lay hidden from our view,  
The supreme Vedic treasure of which we had no clue,  
Harboring thoughts of happiness, we thought we knew the goal,  
But in pampering the body, we never satisfied the soul.  
Blind lead the blind, so-called leaders are dead,  
Busy making plans, but no one knows what lies ahead.  
Another change of body and another death to come,  
Struggling for existence still the fight is never won.  
Lo, behold! The science of the soul is now available to all,  
To be personally experienced by those who heed Lord Caitanya’s call,  
Through the causeless mercy of Your Divine Grace, your words have shown Caitanya’s way,  
And to follow your divine instructions means to always have you stay.Jaya Prabhupāda, Jaya Gurudeva!

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracārine*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

In this Vyāsa-pūjā offering, it seems I can only lament, full of self-pity at the feeble results of my dilettantish acceptance of your order to preach here. “Build a stronghold of Kṛṣṇa consciousness in Nepal,” you had once told us.

You so boldly accepted the order of your Guru Mahārāja and the *paramparā* and executed perfectly Kṛṣṇa’s divine will, giving up your last breath in Kṛṣṇa’s service. Now your sincere followers are following in your footsteps, filling in what you have structured, and I feel struck with wonder at their purity and loyal strength. Now you are manifesting all your divine qualities in your GBC and your empowered *ācāryas,* and if you, through them, can inspire and bless us, then there is hope that we can fulfill your mission here in the land of the Kirāṭas.

Through this descending process on this Vyāsa-pūjā day we try to humbly take our proper places to receive your blessings to preach with renewed enthusiasm, to satisfy you and save all those that you have placed in our care.

*Your most fallen servants,*

Prabhaviṣṇu Swami / Mahā-Viṣṇu dāsa / Dhruvanātha dāsa / Bhaktisiddhānta dāsa / Mādhāi dāsa / Haṁsagati dāsa / Phā1guna dāsa / Bhāṣyakāra dāsa / Sukarmā dāsa / Svarūpa Dāmodara dāsa / Yuga-dharma dāsa / Sudeṣṇa dāsa / Haṁsa-rūpa dāsa / Śaṅkara-cakra dāsa / Rāgānuga-bhakti dāsa / Bhakti-ratnā-devī dāsī

## Kisumu

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracārine*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

It is most amazing that the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Kṛṣṇa, who has immense potencies, who is the reservoir of all qualities, who is one without a second and the resting place of all living entities, has become purchased and bound up by you with a tie of affection which cannot be broken throughout all eternity.

It is amazing that this same Supreme Personality of Godhead is being freely given to the living entities in this world by you, exactly befitting the mood of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, the most merciful incarnation Of Godhead. Your mercy being bestowed upon us in this way cannot be repaid. Yet at the price of humility, we may try to gain some appreciation of you who are an ocean of mercy.

Your mercy is always growing and expanding without limit, as you are the mercy incarnation of Lord Kṛṣṇa. Beholding you in this merciful aspect, we are seeing how you are giving us an ocean of *sādhu-saṅga,* and that devotional association continues to grow more and more, as you are continuing to push on this ISKCON movement. With your appointment of the ISKCON initiating *gurus,* we were encouraged to understand the dynamic principle of ISKCON, which is to become a servant to the Vaiṣṇava community in serving the conditioned souls in becoming free from the cycle of birth and death. You have shown us that this is accomplished with great power, by the loving association of the devotees.

We are encouraged to understand the actual position of a Vaiṣṇava, with humble appreciation. You have even set the example by stating that your disciples were being accepted on behalf of your Guru Mahārāja, Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī, and that unless he had produced you, that you could have done nothing. We are also hoping to gain this same dependence and understanding. We are also seeing by your mercy that the mission of Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī is being specifically carried out by your formation of the GBC, which is to his great pleasure. Because you have arranged this movement in this specific manner, we are obliged to see that even in times of difficulty you are forcefully continuing to push it on.

By your direction of us, who were fallen, we have been engaged by you in the work of delivering the fallen, even as we are being delivered from our fallen condition.

Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, continue to help us in this dense, dark age of Kali, and give us your eternal blessings, as we remain in the shelter of ISKCON.

From ISKCON Kisumu,

Rāmakeli dāsa adhikārī / Bhūtabhāvana dāsa adhikārī / Bhakta Forbes / Bhakta Lukas / Bhakta Antonio / Śyāmasundara / Narottama / Gaurī-devī dāsī / Guṇavatī-devī dāsī / Śakti-mātā-devī dāsī / Govardhana-līlā-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Kitty

## Lagos

**Our Life Shall Be Sublime**

That realm of existence, filled with eternity, knowledge, and bliss,  
Is something of an illusion for those who’ve seen amiss.  
Having independence and not being forced at all,  
But by the force of one’s own desire, came that calamitous fall.  
Caught in māyā’s network and hurled from birth to birth,  
But ahh..., by the will of providence; human life on this earth.  
A chance to see Śrī Kṛṣṇa, by Śrī Caitanya’s grace,  
To hear a bona fide spiritual master; to see his smiling face.  
The order of the spiritual master: surely a means of success.  
In fulfilling that order there is no doubt that one certainly becomes deathless.  
Sādhu-saṅga sādhu-saṅga; asking something confidential  
To open one’s eyes, to see the truth, to reveal one’s mind’s essential.  
Offering gifts and receiving them, offering and accepting prasāda,  
Such wonderful acts given to us by our beloved Prabhupāda.  
To cross the material ocean; deliverance from the wild:  
The action of the spiritual master who’s actually Kṛṣṇa’s child.  
To please the spiritual master is deliverance from the fog.  
Much more success, surely guaranteed, pleasing the spiritual master’s dog.  
The disciples of the guru—to please them is a must.  
The pleasure of the spiritual master is to act in love and trust.  
Prabhupāda’s ISKCON removed us ftom the mire.  
We’re blessed to push on this movement according to his desire.

All glories to His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who continues to push on this movement with great strength and mercy.

*Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare*  
*Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare*

## Laguna Beach

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya*  
*jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena*  
*tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

Vaiṣṇava Ṭhākura, it is certainly worthwhile to take shelter of you, because you are *āśraya-vigraha,* or the manifestation of the Lord whom one must take shelter of. Only you were fired-up enough to come to this side of the world and live among us, to patiently teach us the Vedic knowledge with all its intricacies and give us second birth. In every birth we had a father and mother, but now we have gotten the benediction of Kṛṣṇa and *guru,* so we can conquer the material nature and return home, back to Godhead.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, may I always live with your devotees and in this way serve you under their protection and direction so that you may be pleased. Only you can take us to the other side, or the life of freedom. We are suffering simply because of ignorance, but by strictly following your instructions the darkness of ignorance is removed, and thus we can go to the side of freedom.

## Lake Huntington

To His Divine Grace Oṁ Viṣṇupāda Paramahaṁsa Parivrājakācārya Aṣṭottara-śata Śrī Śrīmad A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Mahārāja Jagad-guru Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. Please accept this humble offering on your most auspicious Vyāsa-pūjā day.

Walking, softly chanting, across the frozen dirt road  
Four A.M (“Ungodly hour,” the materialists goad.)  
Entering the quaint, rustic, wooden-walled room,  
Factually avoiding spiritual doom.  
Maṅgala-ārati! How is it possible that we know?  
O Prabhupāda, only because you have told us so!  
Two unblinking eyes, outstretched arms and a great smile,  
Incense and camphor in a million-year-old style,  
The manifest form of the eternal Supreme Lord,  
An entranceway to where love of Godhead is stored.  
Arcana! How is it possible that we know?  
O Prabhupāda, only because you have told us so!  
The sages question and answer, listen, five thousand years ago.  
Kali-yuga’s beacon the path of perfection does show  
The same questions and answers, listening in the Catskills  
His children and grandchildren with transcendental knowledge he fills. *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam!* How is it possible that we know?  
O Prabhupāda, only because you have told us so!  
Dreams of burfi, Śrīkand, cardamom sweet rice,  
But even cracked wheat and hot milk is so nice.  
Engagement for our tongue so tastefully done,  
Our hearts through our bellies you cent percent won.  
Prasādam! How is it possible that we know?  
O Prabhupāda, only because you have told us so!  
Off to the garden, the barn, the guru-kula,  
A ragtag army of drunks, hippies, and fools.  
Thoughts of Kṛṣṇa lost amid a raucous mental din,  
But by guru-kṛpā, somehow you force your way in.  
Devotional service! How is it possible that we know?  
O Prabhupāda, only because you have told us so!  
Lord Brahmā and Śiva gather at the setting of the sun.  
Amid karatālas and mṛdaṅgas Gaurāṅga’s ārati begun.  
Your children and grandchildren are performing the rite,  
Transformed into Vaikuṇṭha men by your spiritual might.  
Sandhyā-ārati! How is it possible that we know?  
O Prabhupāda, only because you have told us so!  
Oh, most lamentable position, your manifest pastimes end.  
Bewildered and lost, your flock—who will tend?  
“I’m here in my books, instructions, my GBC board,  
Eternally guiding your life as master and lord.”  
Back to Godhead! How is it possible we know?  
O Prabhupāda, only because you have told us so!

Your aspiring-to-be-worthy disciples and granddisciples at  
New Puruṣottama Kṣetra (ISKCON of Lake Huntington)

## La Paz

My dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances. For all of us, you are our life and soul.

Each year it is more difficult to make this Vyāsa-pūjā offering. Each time it is more difficult to express the greatness of your glory. You are the unconquered hero in this fight against *māyā.* To be able to serve you is our greatest glory! Not even the great benefits given by all the demigods together can compare to one drop of your mercy. Your magnanimity knows no bounds—allowing us to associate with you, accepting our service.

Every day your presence extends more on this planet through your books, which descend directly from the spiritual sky like heavenly manna, saving the conditioned souls from the ignorance in this Kali age. By your grace, the light of knowledge and the chanting of the holy name is reaching every town and village.

At the beginning, no one could understand your plans for serving Kṛṣṇa. However, you put confidence in us to participate with you in the service to your spiritual master and Kṛṣṇa, in the shape of ISKCON. How glorious is this movement, and how glorious are those who engage in it!

With huge steps, the world is running to suicide, just as you announced. But by your divine grace, all the conditioned souls are having the opportunity of changing their wretched destination for Goloka Vṛndāvana, Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Without your mercy no activity can be appropriately developed. As you always said, “Without Kṛṣṇa, all of our activities are zero! But if you put the number one in front \[Kṛṣṇa\], immediately you have ten, one hundred, thousands, millions, . . .”

O Śrīla Prabhupāda! Our only ambition at present is to do something capable of satisfying you! Our present hope is to become aspiring servants at your lotus feet. Give us that chance! Who could even imagine what the spiritual world is, before your glorious appearance and your preaching in these Western countries?

O Śrīla Prabhupāda! We have no other shelter than Your Divine Grace.

*Your servants of Bolivia Mandira,*

## Lautoka

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracārine*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We, your disciples and granddisciples in Fiji, are hereby endeavoring to delineate your unlimited greatness by our most futile attempts. Although your physical presence is no longer available to us, you have become a part of our very lives, and not a day passes without associating with your transcendental name, your books, your temples, or your instructions—everything that promotes Kṛṣṇa consciousness simply reminds us of you.

You have mentioned that a Vaiṣṇava is the most elevated personality, and his prominent quality is his touchstone ability to convert the most fallen souls into devotees. You are so overflowing with this quality that even in your physical absence you are converting people to Vaiṣṇavism, by the means of your transcendental gifts of spiritual literatures and the devotees’ preaching activities.

May we be fortunate enough to celebrate your transcendental appearance day, so that with ever-new enthusiasm we can serve your mission of spreading Lord Caitanya’s movement all over the world.

## London

In the darkest Kali-yuga night  
Śrīla Prabhupāda’s books are here  
to give us light.  
Other gurus, sādhus, and wise men illumine us by their firefly’s glimmering flight.  
How can they destroy the darkness of our plight?  
Vyavasāyātmikā *buddhiḥ*—it’s only Prabhupāda  
Who is fit to give us sight.  
He who has thefull connection can alone destroy our night.  
Dear friends, please accept Śrīla Prabhupāda’s books in full,  
Not just a convenience for your sentiment.  
But please analyze the whole content—  
Every word, every argument, each example is for all of us the truth permanent.  
Ācāryavān puruṣo veda  
From whatever place you look,  
In whatever way you see,  
For you and me Śrīla Prabhupāda’s words are always true and relevant!  
So why do you go elsewhere to hear?  
Don’t you know by now what our Prabhupāda would say?  
In your heart you know he’d just chastise you.  
Be chaste to the Jagad-guru! Don’t be a prostitute  
Who accepts another in the presence of her husband.  
Yes, in the presence of our lord and master, Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
Our only real friend.  
He’s not gone away, you know—  
Let his words and orders clothe your nakedness  
On these dark Kali-yuga days.  
For the storm is fast upon us, the furious tempest.  
Quick, dear brothers, be swift to accost your sails and batten the hatches.  
Catch the favorable wind of his words and books;  
Worship that sound, the time’s so short;  
Prabhupāda’s our life and soul, our only strength.  
Are his arguments too simple, too fundamental for you?  
A foul gust is also swirling, trying to shift our faith.  
Don’t be proud, dear friends; there’s nothing more.  
Have you learned the first lesson?  
Are you following the first rules?  
By humility and meekness one attracts the attention of Lord Kṛṣṇa.  
Don’t fall to become once more a mouse.  
Remember Prabhupāda! Remember our Lord!  
Remember your offerings, your vows and prayers!  
He’s the only one who came to save us.  
Who else even knew! Who else really cared!  
He’s the only one who cared—give credit where credit is due. We’re meant to be Prabhupādānugas, are we not?  
For anything else, however pleasant, we care not for that one tiny jot.

## Long Island

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracārine*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

How awkward to approach you with these words fraught with multiple impurities, as they come from the pen of a sinful person. Yet in your divine presence I have become breathless. All of my subterfuge, all my superficial verbal strategies are stunned by your relentless gravity. All my rigid systems of illusion are melted by the sunlight of your intense, genuine love for all of Lord Kṛṣṇa’s eternal parts. Despite my wretched, separatist mentality, you bathe me in your divine gaze and draw me out of this nescience, uplifting me to the spiritual kingdom. In a chastened mood I therefore proceed to offer some homage on this auspicious day of your appearance.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, on behalf of all the members of Śrī Śrī Nimāi-Nitāi Mandira, I wish to place words of love and gratitude at your lotus feet. Thanking you, we offer our *daṇḍavats,* and loving you, we solemnly resolve to cooperate with your worldwide ISKCON family of *rūpānugas.* Preaching Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s message, we shall cojointly spread the chanting of Kṛṣṇa’s glories, especially the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra.* We also vow to use all our faculties to greatly increase the massive distribution of your books, frankly (shamefully) acknowledging some recent setbacks.

I would also like to place at your lotus feet our determination to bring new *bhaktas* into the ranks of Lord Caitanya’s transcendental army. As I introduce them to your empowered sons for initiation, I know that you approve. Let us expose more and more dreadfully conditioned souls to the zest of becoming liberated in the Supreme; let them experience your matchless gifts of love of God. Let them come, let them flourish, let them remain and themselves become great instruments of your mercy, carrying on in *paramparā.*

Moreover, we pray for the power to fulfill a simple pledge: let us *please* you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for you have given us everything, including our actual life. Let us manifest some appreciation of your instruction that this human form should be a bid for eternality, bliss, and knowledge. But let us not be so cripple-minded as to apply the wisdom only to ourselves. If your compassionate message has at all permeated our hearts, then we should be more than willing to distribute it to every suffering soul choking in the noxious atmosphere of Kali.

The path is clear: temples to be opened, books to be distributed, devotees to be made. This is our *bhakti-mārga,* our formula for pleasing almighty Lord Kṛṣṇa by pleasing Your Divine Grace, the Supreme Personality of Servitor Godhead. Let us all (whose spiritual life is living proof of your divinely glorious power) never become so foolish as to look anywhere else for Kṛṣṇa but to your own effulgent, fragrant, and cooling lotus feet.

*Your servants at the Long Island (Freeport) Mandira,*

## Los Angeles

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracārine*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

You have been so merciful to allow me to associate with the wonderful devotees here in New Dvārakā. So somehow because of their transcendental association I have been inspired to write a poetic offering for your pleasure. Please accept this meager offering from all the servants of Śrī Śrī Rukmiṇī-Dvārakādhīśa at your Western world headquarters.

Over the mountains, across the sea,  
Our love and devotion for you stretch to eternity.  
You teach us of our Lord Kṛṣṇa above,  
And now we, too, have life and love.  
Spiritual philosophy and transcendental bliss,  
You are our source of all of this.  
Our material body is nothing but stool,  
But now we think of you as our golden rule.  
Byyour mercy we are allowed to dance and chant,  
And so encourage those who say, “I can’t.”  
Our master and guide will always be you.  
The scriptures state this to be eternally true.  
Repaying our debt can never be done,  
But trying and trying is all the fun.  
Preaching is the essence, the name of the game,  
And in this service we pray to forever remain.

## Madrid

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

In 1975, just after Gaura-pūrṇimā, when Śrī Śrī Kṛṣṇa and Balarāma manifested Their eternal forms on the altar of the newly completed ISKCON *mandira* in Vṛndāvana, there was a great procession to celebrate the occasion. Hundreds of devotees from all corners of the earth assembled to perform ecstatic *kīrtana.* Well-decorated horses and elephants, brass bands, *kīrtana* parties playing various musical instruments, attractively dressed *vrajavāsīs,* various *rathas* and sages of all descriptions all proceeded together, creating a festival for the spiritual senses. And the controller of our spiritual senses was you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, riding your victory chariot, mercifully bestowing your transcendental glance upon all who were fortunate enough to be present. As the parade moved along the road (which was later to be named Bhaktivedanta Marg, to recognize the fact that if anyone wants to enter Vṛndāvana, the topmost planet in the spiritual world, he must go through the gateway of your mercy) from Raman Reti to Vṛndāvana, you appeared to be the full moon decorated by thousands of stars with wonderfully-formed clouds passing in all directions. Here must be the proprietor of Vṛndāvana!! Then suddenly, by your sweet independent will, you decided to proceed on foot. Gracefully, you floated to the ground while the devotees who surrounded you all panicked upon seeing the surging crowds threatening to crush you in their eagerness to achieve your holy *darśana.* We all linked arms to form two long human barricades, one on either side of you, to protect you from the crowd. The energy created by the rushing crowd was tumultuous, like a hurricane; but, as the barricades on either side of you shifted, bringing us closer to you, it was as if we had entered the eye of the hurricane. Such a serene, but ecstatic atmosphere emanated from you as you floated down the road, that we were all transported from our fearful condition. Time seemed to stand still as we moved toward Vṛndāvana, occasionally being pulled into the eye of the hurricane, to relish basking in the warm, effulgent rays of your ecstatic love for Kṛṣṇa.

*bhavad-vidhā bhāgavatās*  
*tīrtha-bhūtāḥ svayaṁ vibho*  
*tīrthī-kurvanti tīrthāni*  
*svāntaḥ-sthena gadābhṛtā*

**“My lord, devotees like your good self are verily holy places personified. Because you carry the Personality of Godhead within your heart, you turn all places into places of pilgrimage.”**

You have created order from the chaos of this Kali-yuga, dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda. We therefore pray to your lordship to accept us as your fully surrendered servants. Please be merciful to us and give us shelter in the shade of your transcendental loving service.

Later, during a morning walk, you were approaching the front gate of the Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma Mandira when suddenly you stopped, pointing your walking stick to a pile of broken pots and other debris in the farthest corner of our property. “What is that?” you challenged. The managers answered that there were extra ingredients which were either damaged or not needed in the decoration of the temple for the Deity installation ceremony. As the different leaders pointed to one another to avoid taking the guilt, you interrupted with a lionlike voice, “You are all just like parrots!! Every morning you sing, *śrṅgāra-tan-mandira-marjanādau:* ‘the spiritual master is always engaged along with his disciples in cleansing the temple.’ And yet you can’t even see that this is also the temple.” As usual, after a stern instruction from your lotus lips there was a long silence. The quality of sound emanating from your voice had a tremendous effect on everyone’s consciousness. You said that the chanting of the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra* sprung from the spiritual stratum. So your voice, which was Kṛṣṇa personally dictating, had the effect of changing matter to spirit instantly. After some time of walking in silence, somehow or other someone was prompted to “break the ice.” “Śrīla Prabhupāda, .. .” “Yes,” you answered sharply. “We all seem to be so foolish and incompetent. How can we help you push on when you are the only one who can always see everything?” You stopped, turned, raised your walking stick, and pointing it at our chests you said, softly but very gravely, “Yes, therefore you must follow your Guru Mahārāja.”

We simply pray to you that you bless us so that the full import of that instruction and the ability to follow it will be manifest in our hearts. Without your mercy it is not possible. We are completely incapable of following you, and yet we are hoping to be able to follow some lifetime. That hope is very painful, because we know we are unable to do it.

You’ve given us everything, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Now it is up to us to take it. By actually following your example, the only thing we have to lose is our miserable condition in this material world, and still we cling to that miserable condition.

This miserly condition of the contaminated heart tends to force us to hoard our energy in a vain attempt to enjoy. You never hoarded your energy, Śrīla Prabhupāda. You gave fully and freely to everyone whatever Kṛṣṇa gave you. A transcendental fountain was thus created by your intense love for Kṛṣṇa and for every living being.

Our hearts are dry and hard—there is no love there. Therefore, we are simply begging at your lotus feet that you force us to accept your mercy. Don’t ever kick us away. Keep us in the association of your pure devotees. Please let us chant Hare Kṛṣṇa.

*Your loving disciples and granddisciples from the Spanish Yātrā,*

## Malaysia

**An Offering to Our Beloved Spiritual Master**

I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, by whose mercy the dumb can recite beautiful poetry, the lame can cross mountains, and the blind can see stars in the sky.

We were lost in this bewildering world, confused and frightened here.  
The more we tried to enjoy and understand, the more we seemed to find danger and fear.  
Everywhere we looked we found trouble—birth, death, disease and old age.  
“Where to turn? Sinking deeper and deeper, all around us like actors on a stage.  
Somehow we couldn’t find that satisfaction, but just kept seeking on and on.  
The Supreme Lord, Kṛṣṇa, saw our pain and sent you, Prabhupāda, out of compassion.  
You cared not for personal comfort, gave up family, friends and home.  
Thinking always of your great mission, around the world, fearless, you did roam.  
I’ll never forget the first time I saw you, with golden form shining bright,  
Glowing with unbounded love and compassion to force open our eyes with your knowledge torchlight.  
You gave us love, you gave us life, revealed to us the real world.  
Freed us from danger, trouble and strife and God’s service banner unfurled.  
You gave us chanting of the holy name and Bhagavad-gītā to relish.  
*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* reveals the pastimes and fame of Lord Kṛṣṇa, who’ll never perish.  
You gave us service to the Supreme Lord so we can dovetail our propensities.  
Unlimited forms and ways to serve, ever increasing in ecstatic bliss.  
Kṛṣṇa-jayanti, Ratha-yātrā, transcendental pastimes of the Lord,  
So we can engage our restless minds and free our thoughts from this world.  
So much you gave us selflessly—how can we repay the debt?  
We pray that our service may be pleasing to you and our offenses you’ll forgive and forget.

Please help us to become selfless servants engaged in Caitanya’s mission So we may see this movement expanding, revealing full mercy to everyone’s vision.

*From your unworthy but fortunate servants in Malaysia,*

## Manipur

Most beloved Srila Prabhupāda,

We offer our most humble *praṇāmas* at your divine lotus feet. With the opening of the Bhaktivedanta Institute Mission Schools in Manipur and Tirupati early this year, we are beginning to see the potency of your transcendental instruction about presenting the science of Kṛṣṇa consciousness to the scholarly and responsible community of the human society. One unique feature in Manipur is that most of the intelligent parents are withdrawing their children from Christian missionary schools and are bringing them to our school. These parents are the most respected people. They are educators, administrators, etc. Under ordinary situations these people are not very much attracted to associate with the devotees. However, since the opening of the Bhaktivedanta Institute Mission Schools, all these important souls and their friends are regularly visiting our temple. They freely mix with the devotees, take *prasādam*and participate in various transcendental programs. They become familiar with the worldwide activities of ISKCON, and they become very enthusiastic about helping the mission of ISKCON through the medium of education. Most importantly, all these children are getting the highest standard of education with a solid Kṛṣṇa conscious foundation from the very beginning. A good percentage of these children will become fulitime devotees when they finish their education, and they will be the best leaders in all fields of human activities.

In Tirupati all the important leaders in the community appreciate such a school. They are helping the Bhaktivedanta Institute programs in various ways.

Your Divine Grace was extremely concerned about the present system of education. Because modern education is based on a materialistic outlook on life, it cannot be expected to provide the principles necessary to develop higher knowledge of morality, ethics or even philosophy and religion. Yet these things are certainly more important than the gadgets produced by science and technology. Somehow a gap has been created between science and religion, and it must be filled up. A synthesis has to be made in order to bring body, mind and spirit together as a unified whole. Present education merely rips these real aspects of existence apart. In fact, materialism, which often parades as secularism, not only neglects but annihilates the spiritual dimension of man. The Bhaktivedanta Mission School system is meant to correct this imbalanced education by providing for the student a well-rounded understanding of life from a perspective which includes the best of modern education along with the highest spiritual training that is so necessary for personal development. This is the first educational system which really gives the student a chance to understand the full spectrum of life in all its aspects. This system of education is designed to produce future leaders of society who will know the progressive values of life.

Since the modern system of education, especially science education, regards life as a completely material phenomenon, we see today a great breakdown of moral and ethical principles in all sections of the society.

Bhaktivedanta Institute Mission Schools emphasize the importance of the spiritual dimension of life. This is in accord with the information found in the time-honored scriptures of the world, which have formed the foundation for the lives of the most noble-minded individuals in the history of human civilization.

The Bhaktivedanta Institute mission is to create the best-educated class of men and women who can understand the essential meaning and purpose of life. In order to achieve this aim, it is necessary to create a healthy atmosphere in modern society by making such schools available throughout the world. These schools will train children from a very young age, since the impressions made in a child’s formative years have a lasting effect which stays throughout his life. Hence these schools will fill the gap left by modern education and will provide a sound basis for the development of a civilized society for modern man, free from quarrelsomeness, hatred and godlessness, thus making the world a peaceful place for everyone.

Another unique feature of these schools will be the introduction of *bhakti*as a practical science which can be used for understanding the ultimate aim of life. Children should be formally trained to give respect to teachers, elders and parents, as well as how to be clean, inwardly and outwardly, and to be completely free from the influence of bad habits such as smoking, intoxication, gambling and improper conduct in the relation between boys and girls. Most importantly, they will be trained how to develop devotion for duty and love for God, the ultimate basis of the noblest qualities of life.

We would also like to create a real spirit of missionary zeal among our students in order to protect, preserve and spread the science of *Bhagavad-gītā* and *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* the foundation of *sanātana-dharma* throughout the world.

The aims and objectives of the Bhaktivedanta Institute Mission Schools are:

1. To bring out and develop the innate qualities of the child to the fullest.
2. To provide an educational environment favorable for the all around development of body, mind and spirit of the students.
3. To cultivate the consciousness that over and above the concerns of physical well-being, spiritual reawakening is the real goal of education.
4. To produce noble-minded persons responsible for the moral and spiritual welfare of society.
5. To create the understanding that the science of devotion to the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, is the basis for success in all aspects of life.

By your divine mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda, our students are doing extremely well. Besides learning other academic subjects, all of them are learning *moral and spiritual science:* the basic science of respect, worship, obedience, cleanliness, truthfulness, kindness, nonviolence, and prayer to the spiritual masters, the goddess of learning and to Śrīla Vyāsadeva, very intently. All are also chanting the *mahā-mantra:* Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare / Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare with great enthusiasm.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for kindly engaging us in your divine mission. Our only shelter is your divine lotus feet.

*Your insignificant servants,*

## Śrī Māyāpur

All glories to Your Divine Grace, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

That most wonderful occasion of the celebration of your divine appearance has once again come; the climax of another year of life given in your service; the day upon which we can come together in each of your ISKCON centers to glorify and remember you.

You have created a vast and glorious society and gigantic spiritual body which lives and breathes the life which you have given it, and all of its different facets are manifestations of your own personality, desires, and instructions. One who lives in this, your ISKCON society, cannot fail to remember you from the moment he rises to the time he takes rest at night.

As we chant our *japa,* we realize that this was your gift to us, and we remember our vows to you. It is you who we glorify in our pre-dawn *kīrtanas.* Class is your divine instructions from the books you dedicated your life to writing. *Prasādam* is your mercy (even the knowledge of how to cook, you gave us). We can only preach by calling upon the vast ocean of knowledge from which you have spoonfed us, and thus we are united with you.

From the very beginning, when there were no installed Deities, you attracted us, the most sinful, degraded creatures, to your divine shelter by the way you walked, your head held high, the way you scratched your ear or nose, your looks of surprise and affection, your little anecdotes about everything and everyone from Alexander to Gandhi or your own childhood, by the way you distributed *prasādam*—that incomparable *prasādam!* And by your endless patience, enthusiasm and words of encouragement, you caught us up in the nets of attachment to you.

Now your society has grown so large, but still within it we see nothing but you. Your dear disciples, your GBC men, are guiding us day by day, and through them we are able to appreciate your presence more and more in our various services.

Here in Śrī Māyāpur Chandrodaya Mandir, we are so fortunate to be blessed with the association of Bhavānanda Goswami and Jayapatāka Swami, whose unfailing love for you is truly inspiring to us all. You have trained them so carefully, and as first-class sons they are representing you perfectly. We simply pray that we remain eternally in your service and in some way help to expand your glories, ISKCON’s glories, all over the world.

You have said, “There is no question of Kṛṣṇa consciousness outside of ISKCON.” That realization fills our hearts, for “outside of ISKCON” means outside of you. Except for you and your bona fide representatives, no one knows how to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness. This has been seen. So we are simply pleading with you that we may always remain eternally engaged in service to your lotus feet and in the service of those great souls who have fully taken shelter of you. We see nothing more worthy of aspiration in all the fourteen planetary systems.

*Your insignificant servants from Śrī Māyāpur Chandrodaya Mandir*

## Medellin

Dear, most worshipable Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our worthless obeisances at your lotus feet. The relationship of a disciple with his spiritual master is as good as his relationship with Kṛṣṇa.

*ācāryaṁ māṁ vijānīyān*  
*nāvanmanyeta karhicit*  
*na martya-buddhyāsūyeta*  
*sarva-deva-mayo guruḥ*

**“One should know the** ***ācārya*** **as Myself and never disrespect him in any way. One should not envy him, thinking him an ordinary man, for he is** ***sarva-devamaya,*** **the representative of all the demigods.”**

Just as a living entity in the material world gives the power of attorney to his mind, Kṛṣṇa similarly invests His multipotencies in a pure soul like you. Among many millions who are liberated and perfect in knowledge of liberation, one may be a pure devotee of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

The *pāṣāṇḍis* sometimes accuse the Hare Kṛṣṇa movement of being not bona fide. Despite all the activities of these *pāṣāṇḍis*, however, the prediction of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu, which was handed down by you, will triumph.

*pṛhivīte āche yata nagarādi grāma*  
*sarvatra pracāra haibe mora nāma*

**“In every town and village, the chanting of My name will be heard.” No one can check the spread of your movement, Śrīla Prabhupāda, because upon it is the benediction of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu.**

Please allow us to remain your *astras* (weapons)!

## Melbourne

**“Sent by God”**

O rapturous master of our memories,  
An earnest sweetness you did dispense.  
O loving lord, Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
How is it, ask we, your appearance—mercy!  
Which caused your pastimes to commence.  
Surely you were sent by God.  
O splendid moment lost in depths of time,  
Recovered now by your armies great.  
O lord of light, merciful savior,  
We now can recall that infant so small,  
His life to displace mankind’s fate,  
Accomplished by his pure behavior.  
How fortunate that jack-fruit tree protecting  
The blessed fruit of Sri Gour Mohan De.  
O beautiful gem—bestower of bliss—  
Rajani Ma beaming at “Moti,” so gleaming.  
In humbleness you grew to light the way.  
What could be more wondrous than this?  
Alas, how queer, it seems the world doth dangle  
A plague of mysteries before our sight.  
Ah, gracious guide, Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
Your appearance most rare, as a babe without cares,  
Stuns us in our day and night.  
Surely you were sent by God.

## Mexico

Prabhupāda, your body is ISKCON;  
who would ever want to leave you?

Prabhupāda, you’re always alive;  
your books are evidence of this.

Prabhupāda, your hands are your disciples (and their disciples)  
who are working throughout the whole world.

Prabhupāda, in your eyes is Kṛṣṇaloka;  
you are seeing everything there is to know.

Prabhupāda, your gift is the holy name;  
who could be more magnanimous than you?

Prabhupāda, your desire, is that we go back to Godhead;  
you are saving the whole world from illusion.

Prabhupāda, your weapons are the scriptures;  
thus you protect the devotees and defeat the demons.

Prabhupāda, you sing the Bhāgavatam;  
masterfully you are enchanting the world.

Prabhupāda, you gave us the Caitanya-caritāmṛta,  
which is the sweetest of all nectars.

Prabhupāda, you are present in your instructions;  
your books are the guides for future generations.

Prabhupāda, you are pleased by our saṅkīrtana;  
who would not want to please you?

Prabhupāda, your torchlight is the BBT,  
thus the way becomes clear.

Prabhupāda, you are shouting through book distribution;  
your voice is heard in the most remote corners of the world.

Prabhupāda, your inspiration is your Guru Mahārāja;  
your undeviating faith in his order has made you triumphant.

Prabhupāda, you are the deliverer of the three worlds,  
angel of redemption.

Prabhupāda, you are the master of all masters,  
epitome of perfection.

Prabhupāda, your mission is to give Kṛṣṇa to the world;  
Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu has empowered you.

O master of masters! O divine mercy personified! O lasher of irreligion! Conqueror of the three worlds!

Although you have never left us, don’t let us ever forget you!

We humbly pray in the dust of your lotus feet to be your eternal servants,

The devotees of Mexico Mandira,

## Mexico Farm

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracārine*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

The dawn breaks, the sun, with rays of hope of another day of service, appears on the hilltops. And you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, also seemingly appearing from the east, have arisen in our hearts, thus irrevocably driving out the ignorance of our slavish infatuation for the objects of our capricious senses, and giving us a glimpse of your two enchanting lotus feet. I am a most unworthy recipient of your mercy and have no way of ever repaying you, but if you will please give me some time, I will joyfully pay something in installments and surrender myself to serve you minute by minute of endless time. Some may say that I am most naive to surrender myself to you, being young and hardly having experienced the beckoning world. But I would say in reply that since the soul is eternal, I have already repeatedly “chewed the chewed,” and do not want to choke anymore on this mouthful of useless, juiceless pulp. And my mind is tired from so much needless speculation. By Kṛṣṇa’s causeless mercy, now I have somehow or other developed a taste for serving you. In this way, I consider myself most fortunate; please allow me to always serve you.

Here in Nuevo Gaura Mandala Bhumi, Mexico’s first farm community, we want to please you by simple living, high thinking, and plain old hard work. We may not have received any Ph.D.s from the university, but now we are employed in our Plough Department. Whatever we lack in know-how we pray will be compensated for by our determined desire to please you. Please train us as your loyal servitors.

Your fallen servants of Nuevo Gaura Mandala Bhumi,

## Mexico Guru-kula

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracārine*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Adored Śrīla Prabhupāda,

You left your Vṛndāvana and came to the Western countries to bring us Kṛṣṇa consciousness. You were the only one to obey Lord Caitanya when he ordered that this movement be spread to every town and village.Who can understand your power and dominion overthis world? It is stated in the *Vedas, samit-pāṇiḥ śrotriyaṁ brahma-niṣṭham:* “The authorized *guru* is heavy and is always fixed in the Absolute Truth.” You came to the land of the uncivilized and enlightened us with the transcendental torch of knowledge. You have given us new life; therefore you are our true father and well-wisher.

You once said, “I will live forever in my books.” In this way we are destroying our dangerous enemy, illusion, through the words emanating from your lotus mouth. Śrīla Prabhupāda, if it were not for your instructions, there would be no hope for our lives.

On the boat of ISKCON we are navagating the stormy waters of the ocean of material existence, and you are the expert captain who effortlessly guides us on our course. Great *mahātmās* have been able to cross this ocean of material nescience by taking shelter at your lotus feet, and by your mercy this ocean has become reduced to the size of a calf’s hoofprint.

Here at the *guru-kula*(the house of the spiritual master), due to your presence the atmosphere is saintly and transcendental. Please give us the strength to properly honor your house, as it is nondifferent from Goloka Vṛndāvana. Give us the strength to please you at every moment, and to be able to serve you according to your own sweet will. Only through your pure devotees will we be able to please and understand you; therefore we pray never to give up the association of your bona fide servants. Without

such association, the path of spiritual life is very difficult and uncertain. *Janma sat-saṅga* is the most difficult gift to obtain in this rare human form of life.

Here at the *guru-kula* we have the responsibility to enlighten many little devotees; but you are the original torch, and we are pleading for your benediction of purity and strength to execute our transcendental duties successfully.

Your servants at the Mexico Guru-kula,
